/**
 * Canvas Component - Presentation Layer
 * Renders slide with Konva
 */

import React, { useRef, useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Transformer, Line as KonvaLine } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { ElementRenderer } from './ElementRenderer';
import { GridLayer } from './GridLayer';
import { MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM, PORTRAIT_VISUAL_FACTOR, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../../shared/constants';
import { useRubberBand } from '../hooks/useRubberBand';
import { useGuides } from '../hooks/useGuides';
import { computeSnap } from '../../services/snap';

interface CanvasProps {
  slide: Slide;
  selectedIds: readonly string[];
  onSelect: (id: string, multi: boolean, clickPos?: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
  onUpdate: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: React.MouseEvent, elementId: string | null) => void;
  // Fill mode props
  fillMode?: {
    isActive: boolean;
    points: readonly number[];
    color: string;
  };
  onFillClick?: () => boolean;
  onFillDoubleClick?: () => void;
  onPointerMove?: (pos: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
}

export const Canvas: React.FC<CanvasProps> = ({
  slide,
  selectedIds,
  onSelect,
  onUpdate,
  onDelete,
  onContextMenu,
  fillMode,
  onFillClick,
  onFillDoubleClick,
  onPointerMove,
}) => {
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<Konva.Layer>(null);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const [displayZoom, setDisplayZoom] = React.useState(1);
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = React.useState(true);
  const [showMargins, setShowMargins] = React.useState(true);
  const [hoveredId, setHoveredId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  // Rubber band (lasso) selection
  const { rubberBand, handleMouseDown: startRubberBand, handleMouseMove: updateRubberBand, handleMouseUp: endRubberBand, reset: resetRubberBand } = useRubberBand();
  
  // Snap guides
  const { guides, showGuides, clearGuides } = useGuides();

  // Crosshair cursor in fill mode
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (fillMode?.isActive) {
      container.style.cursor = 'crosshair';
    } else {
      container.style.cursor = 'default';
    }
  }, [fillMode?.isActive]);

  const zoom = useMemo(() => {
    const orientationFactor =
      slide.orientation === 'portrait' ? PORTRAIT_VISUAL_FACTOR : 1;
    return displayZoom * orientationFactor;
  }, [displayZoom, slide.orientation]);

  // Sync transformer with selected nodes
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;

    if (!selectedIds.length) {
      tr.nodes([]);
      tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
      return;
    }

    const layers = stage.getLayers();
    const nodes = selectedIds
      .map((id) => {
        for (const layer of layers) {
          const node = layer.findOne('#' + id);
          if (node) return node;
        }
        return null;
      })
      .filter((n): n is Konva.Node => !!n);

    tr.nodes(nodes);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedIds]);

  const handleStageMouseDown = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;
      
      const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
      if (!pointer) return;
      
      const scale = stage.scaleX();
      const pos = { x: pointer.x / scale, y: pointer.y / scale };
      
      if (onPointerMove) {
        onPointerMove(pos);
      }
      
      // Check if clicked on stage (not on element)
      const isStageClick = e.target === e.target.getStage();
      
      // Handle fill mode click
      if (fillMode?.isActive && onFillClick) {
        const handled = onFillClick();
        if (handled) return;
      }
      
      // Start rubber band selection on stage click
      if (isStageClick && !fillMode?.isActive) {
        startRubberBand(pos);
      }
      
      // Only clear selection if clicked on empty stage (not on element)
      if (!isStageClick) return;
      onSelect('', false);
    },
    [onSelect, fillMode, onFillClick, onPointerMove, startRubberBand]
  );

  const handleStageMouseMove = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;
      
      const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
      if (!pointer) return;
      
      const scale = stage.scaleX();
      const pos = { x: pointer.x / scale, y: pointer.y / scale };
      
      if (onPointerMove) {
        onPointerMove(pos);
      }
      
      // Update rubber band
      updateRubberBand(pos);
    },
    [onPointerMove, updateRubberBand]
  );

  const handleStageDoubleClick = useCallback(() => {
    if (fillMode?.isActive && onFillDoubleClick) {
      onFillDoubleClick();
    }
  }, [fillMode, onFillDoubleClick]);

  const handleStageMouseUp = useCallback(() => {
    // End rubber band selection
    const result = endRubberBand();
    if (result) {
      // Select elements inside rubber band rectangle
      const { start, end } = result;
      const minX = Math.min(start.x, end.x);
      const maxX = Math.max(start.x, end.x);
      const minY = Math.min(start.y, end.y);
      const maxY = Math.max(start.y, end.y);
      
      const selected = slide.elements.filter(el => {
        const elRight = el.x + el.width;
        const elBottom = el.y + el.height;
        return el.x >= minX && elRight <= maxX && el.y >= minY && elBottom <= maxY;
      });
      
      selected.forEach(el => onSelect(el.id, true));
    }
  }, [endRubberBand, slide.elements, onSelect]);

  const handleWheel = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => {
      e.evt.preventDefault();

      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;

      const isCtrl = e.evt.ctrlKey || e.evt.metaKey;
      if (!isCtrl) return;

      const oldScale = stage.scaleX();
      const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
      if (!pointer) return;

      const mousePointTo = {
        x: (pointer.x - stage.x()) / oldScale,
        y: (pointer.y - stage.y()) / oldScale,
      };

      const direction = e.evt.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
      const newScale = Math.max(
        MIN_ZOOM,
        Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, oldScale + direction * 0.05)
      );

      stage.scale({ x: newScale, y: newScale });

      const newPos = {
        x: pointer.x - mousePointTo.x * newScale,
        y: pointer.y - mousePointTo.y * newScale,
      };

      stage.position(newPos);
      setDisplayZoom(newScale);
    },
    []
  );

  const handleTransformEnd = useCallback(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    if (!tr) return;

    const nodes = tr.nodes();
    for (const node of nodes) {
      const id = node.id();
      const scaleX = node.scaleX();
      const scaleY = node.scaleY();
      const rotation = node.rotation();

      onUpdate(id, {
        x: node.x(),
        y: node.y(),
        width: Math.max(20, node.width() * scaleX),
        height: Math.max(20, node.height() * scaleY),
        rotation,
      });

      // Reset scale after applying
      node.scaleX(1);
      node.scaleY(1);
    }
  }, [onUpdate]);

  const printableArea = useMemo(() => ({
    x: slide.padding.left,
    y: slide.padding.top,
    width: Math.max(0, slide.width - slide.padding.left - slide.padding.right),
    height: Math.max(0, slide.height - slide.padding.top - slide.padding.bottom),
  }), [slide.padding, slide.width, slide.height]);

  return (
    <Stage
      ref={stageRef}
      width={slide.width * zoom}
      height={slide.height * zoom}
      scaleX={zoom}
      scaleY={zoom}
      onMouseDown={handleStageMouseDown}
      onMouseMove={handleStageMouseMove}
      onMouseUp={handleStageMouseUp}
      onDblClick={handleStageDoubleClick}
      onWheel={handleWheel}
      onMouseLeave={resetRubberBand}
    >
      {/* Background Layer */}
      <Layer listening={false}>
        <Rect
          x={0}
          y={0}
          width={slide.width}
          height={slide.height}
          fill={slide.background}
          shadowColor="#000"
          shadowBlur={10}
          shadowOpacity={0.18}
          shadowOffsetY={2}
        />
        {slide.backgroundImage && (
          <BackgroundImage
            src={slide.backgroundImage}
            width={slide.width}
            height={slide.height}
          />
        )}
        {showGrid && <GridLayer width={slide.width} height={slide.height} />}
        {showMargins && (
          <Rect
            x={printableArea.x}
            y={printableArea.y}
            width={printableArea.width}
            height={printableArea.height}
            stroke="#3b82f6"
            strokeWidth={1}
            dash={[6, 4]}
          />
        )}
        {/* Fill mode: draw polygon line */}
        {fillMode?.isActive && fillMode.points.length >= 2 && (
          <KonvaLine
            points={[...fillMode.points]}
            stroke={fillMode.color}
            strokeWidth={2}
            dash={[6, 4]}
          />
        )}
        {/* Fill mode: draw points */}
        {fillMode?.isActive && fillMode.points.map((_, i) => {
          if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
          return (
            <Rect
              key={i}
              x={fillMode.points[i] - 3}
              y={fillMode.points[i + 1] - 3}
              width={6}
              height={6}
              fill="#6366f1"
              cornerRadius={3}
            />
          );
        })}
        {/* Rubber band selection */}
        {rubberBand.isActive && rubberBand.start && rubberBand.current && (
          <Rect
            x={Math.min(rubberBand.start.x, rubberBand.current.x)}
            y={Math.min(rubberBand.start.y, rubberBand.current.y)}
            width={Math.abs(rubberBand.current.x - rubberBand.start.x)}
            height={Math.abs(rubberBand.current.y - rubberBand.start.y)}
            fill="rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1)"
            stroke="#3b82f6"
            strokeWidth={1}
            dash={[4, 4]}
          />
        )}
        {/* Snap guides */}
        {guides.map((guide, i) => (
          <KonvaLine
            key={i}
            points={guide.orientation === 'horizontal' 
              ? [0, guide.position, slide.width, guide.position]
              : [guide.position, 0, guide.position, slide.height]
            }
            stroke="#ef4444"
            strokeWidth={1}
            dash={[4, 4]}
          />
        ))}
      </Layer>

      {/* Elements Layer */}
      <Layer ref={layerRef}>
        {slide.elements.map((element) => (
          <ElementRenderer
            key={element.id}
            element={element}
            isSelected={selectedIds.includes(element.id)}
            isHovered={hoveredId === element.id}
            onSelect={() => onSelect(element.id, false)}
            onUpdate={(patch) => onUpdate(element.id, patch)}
            onHover={(hovered) => setHoveredId(hovered ? element.id : null)}
            onDragStart={() => clearGuides()}
            onDragMove={(x, y) => {
              // Compute snap guides during drag
              const snap = computeSnap(element, x, y, slide);
              if (snap.v.length > 0 || snap.h.length > 0) {
                showGuides([
                  ...snap.v.map(v => ({ orientation: 'vertical', position: v })),
                  ...snap.h.map(h => ({ orientation: 'horizontal', position: h })),
                ]);
              } else {
                clearGuides();
              }
            }}
            onDragEnd={() => clearGuides()}
          />
        ))}

        <Transformer
          ref={trRef}
          rotateEnabled
          keepRatio={false}
          borderStroke="#f59e0b"
          anchorStroke="#f59e0b"
          anchorFill="#fbbf24"
          anchorSize={10}
          anchorCornerRadius={2}
          rotationSnaps={[0, 15, 30, 45, 60, 75, 90, 105, 120, 135, 150, 165, 180, 195, 210, 225, 240, 255, 270, 285, 300, 315, 330, 345]}
          rotationSnapTolerance={6}
          boundBoxFunc={(oldBox, newBox) =>
            newBox.width < 20 || newBox.height < 20 ? oldBox : newBox
          }
          onTransformEnd={handleTransformEnd}
        />
      </Layer>
    </Stage>
  );
};

Canvas.displayName = 'Canvas';

// Background Image Component
const BackgroundImage: React.FC<{ src: string; width: number; height: number }> = ({
  src,
  width,
  height,
}) => {
  const [image, setImage] = React.useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const img = new window.Image();
    img.src = src;
    img.onload = () => setImage(img);
  }, [src]);

  if (!image) return null;

  return (
    <Rect
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      fillPatternImage={image}
      fillPatternScale={{
        x: width / image.width,
        y: height / image.height,
      }}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

FILE: presentation\components\ContextMenu.tsx
SIZE: 4307 bytes

/**
 * Context Menu Component - Presentation Layer
 * Right-click menu for elements
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

interface ContextMenuProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onCut: () => void;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onPaste: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onCopyStyle: () => void;
  onApplyStyle: () => void;
  canPaste: boolean;
  hasStyleCopied: boolean;
}

export const ContextMenu: React.FC<ContextMenuProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  elementId,
  selectedElements,
  onClose,
  onCut,
  onCopy,
  onPaste,
  onDuplicate,
  onDelete,
  onBringForward,
  onSendBackward,
  onCopyStyle,
  onApplyStyle,
  canPaste,
  hasStyleCopied,
}) => {
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close on click outside
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  // Clamp position to viewport
  const clampedX = Math.min(x, window.innerWidth - 230);
  const clampedY = Math.min(y, window.innerHeight - 300);

  const hasSelection = selectedElements.length > 0;
  const isLine =
    selectedElements.length === 1 && selectedElements[0].type === 'line';

  return (
    <div
      ref={menuRef}
      className="context-menu"
      style={{ left: clampedX, top: clampedY }}
    >
      {hasSelection && (
        <>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCut}>
            <span>Cut</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+X</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopy}>
            <span>Copy</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+C</span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`context-menu-item ${!canPaste ? 'disabled' : ''}`}
            onClick={canPaste ? onPaste : undefined}
          >
            <span>Paste</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onDuplicate}>
            <span>Duplicate</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+D</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopyStyle}>
            <span>Copy Style</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+C</span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`context-menu-item ${!hasStyleCopied ? 'disabled' : ''}`}
            onClick={hasStyleCopied ? onApplyStyle : undefined}
          >
            <span>Apply Style</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onBringForward}>
            <span>Bring Forward</span>
            <span className="shortcut">]</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onSendBackward}>
            <span>Send Backward</span>
            <span className="shortcut">[</span>
          </div>
          {isLine && (
            <>
              <div className="context-menu-divider" />
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Add Point</span>
              </div>
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Remove Last Point</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
        </>
      )}

      <div className="context-menu-item danger" onClick={onDelete}>
        <span>Delete</span>
        <span className="shortcut">Del</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

ContextMenu.displayName = 'ContextMenu';

/**
 * Data Panel Component - Presentation Layer
 * Manage data schema for placeholders
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useSlidesContext } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import type { DataRoot, DataField, DataFieldType } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

export const DataPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const { slides, activeSlide } = useSlidesContext();
  const [data, setData] = useState<DataRoot[]>([]);
  const [expandedRoots, setExpandedRoots] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const toggleRoot = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setExpandedRoots((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const addRoot = useCallback(() => {
    const newRoot: DataRoot = {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'New Root',
      fields: [],
    };
    setData((prev) => [...prev, newRoot]);
    setExpandedRoots((prev) => new Set(prev).add(newRoot.id));
  }, []);

  const updateRootName = useCallback((id: string, name: string) => {
    setData((prev) =>
      prev.map((root) => (root.id === id ? { ...root, name } : root))
    );
  }, []);

  const removeRoot = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setData((prev) => prev.filter((root) => root.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addField = useCallback((rootId: string, parentId?: string) => {
    const newField: DataField = {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'New Field',
      type: 'string',
      sample: '',
    };

    setData((prev) =>
      prev.map((root) => {
        if (root.id !== rootId) return root;

        if (parentId) {
          // Add to parent field (object/array)
          return {
            ...root,
            fields: root.fields.map((field) =>
              field.id === parentId && field.children
                ? { ...field, children: [...field.children, newField] }
                : field
            ),
          };
        }

        return { ...root, fields: [...root.fields, newField] };
      })
    );
  }, []);

  const updateField = useCallback(
    (rootId: string, fieldId: string, patch: Partial<DataField>) => {
      setData((prev) =>
        prev.map((root) => {
          if (root.id !== rootId) return root;
          return {
            ...root,
            fields: root.fields.map((field) =>
              field.id === fieldId ? { ...field, ...patch } : field
            ),
          };
        })
      );
    },
    []
  );

  const removeField = useCallback((rootId: string, fieldId: string) => {
    setData((prev) =>
      prev.map((root) => {
        if (root.id !== rootId) return root;
        return {
          ...root,
          fields: root.fields.filter((field) => field.id !== fieldId),
        };
      })
    );
  }, []);

  const getFieldPath = useCallback(
    (root: DataRoot, field: DataField): string => {
      return `${root.name}.${field.name}`;
    },
    []
  );

  const handleDragStart = useCallback(
    (e: React.DragEvent, root: DataRoot, field: DataField) => {
      e.dataTransfer.setData(
        'application/json',
        JSON.stringify({
          path: getFieldPath(root, field),
          type: field.type,
          sample: field.sample,
        })
      );
    },
    [getFieldPath]
  );

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="data-panel">
        <div className="data-panel-header">
          <h3>Data</h3>
          <button onClick={addRoot} className="btn-add-small">
            + Add Root
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="data-panel-empty">
          <p>No data defined.</p>
          <p>
            Add data roots to use placeholders in your templates.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="data-panel">
      <div className="data-panel-header">
        <h3>Data</h3>
        <button onClick={addRoot} className="btn-add-small">
          + Add Root
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="data-roots">
        {data.map((root) => (
          <DataRootItem
            key={root.id}
            root={root}
            isExpanded={expandedRoots.has(root.id)}
            onToggle={() => toggleRoot(root.id)}
            onUpdateName={(name) => updateRootName(root.id, name)}
            onRemove={() => removeRoot(root.id)}
            onAddField={() => addField(root.id)}
            onUpdateField={(fieldId, patch) =>
              updateField(root.id, fieldId, patch)
            }
            onRemoveField={(fieldId) => removeField(root.id, fieldId)}
            onDragStart={handleDragStart}
            editingId={editingId}
            setEditingId={setEditingId}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface DataRootItemProps {
  root: DataRoot;
  isExpanded: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
  onUpdateName: (name: string) => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
  onAddField: () => void;
  onUpdateField: (fieldId: string, patch: Partial<DataField>) => void;
  onRemoveField: (fieldId: string) => void;
  onDragStart: (
    e: React.DragEvent,
    root: DataRoot,
    field: DataField
  ) => void;
  editingId: string | null;
  setEditingId: (id: string | null) => void;
}

const DataRootItem: React.FC<DataRootItemProps> = ({
  root,
  isExpanded,
  onToggle,
  onUpdateName,
  onRemove,
  onAddField,
  onUpdateField,
  onRemoveField,
  onDragStart,
  editingId,
  setEditingId,
}) => {
  const isEditing = editingId === root.id;

  return (
    <div className="data-root">
      <div className="data-root-header">
        <button className="data-toggle" onClick={onToggle}>
          {isExpanded ? 'в–ј' : 'в–¶'}
        </button>
        {isEditing ? (
          <input
            type="text"
            value={root.name}
            onChange={(e) => onUpdateName(e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => setEditingId(null)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter') setEditingId(null);
            }}
            className="data-name-input"
            autoFocus
          />
        ) : (
          <span
            className="data-root-name"
            onDoubleClick={() => setEditingId(root.id)}
          >
            {root.name}
          </span>
        )}
        <button className="data-remove" onClick={onRemove}>
          Г—
        </button>
      </div>

      {isExpanded && (
        <div className="data-fields">
          {root.fields.map((field) => (
            <DataFieldItem
              key={field.id}
              root={root}
              field={field}
              onUpdate={(patch) => onUpdateField(field.id, patch)}
              onRemove={() => onRemoveField(field.id)}
              onDragStart={onDragStart}
            />
          ))}
          <button onClick={onAddField} className="data-add-field">
            + Add Field
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

interface DataFieldItemProps {
  root: DataRoot;
  field: DataField;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<DataField>) => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
  onDragStart: (
    e: React.DragEvent,
    root: DataRoot,
    field: DataField
  ) => void;
}

const DataFieldItem: React.FC<DataFieldItemProps> = ({
  root,
  field,
  onUpdate,
  onRemove,
  onDragStart,
}) => {
  const typeColors: Record<DataFieldType, string> = {
    string: '#3b82f6',
    number: '#10b981',
    date: '#f59e0b',
    boolean: '#8b5cf6',
    image: '#ef4444',
    object: '#6b7280',
    array: '#d97706',
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="data-field"
      draggable
      onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, root, field)}
    >
      <div className="data-field-row">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={field.name}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ name: e.target.value })}
          className="data-field-name"
        />
        <select
          value={field.type}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ type: e.target.value as DataFieldType })}
          className="data-field-type"
          style={{ color: typeColors[field.type] }}
        >
          <option value="string">string</option>
          <option value="number">number</option>
          <option value="date">date</option>
          <option value="boolean">boolean</option>
          <option value="image">image</option>
          <option value="object">object</option>
          <option value="array">array</option>
        </select>
        <button className="data-remove" onClick={onRemove}>
          Г—
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="data-field-path">
        {'{{'}
        {root.name}.{field.name}
        {'}}'}
      </div>
      <input
        type="text"
        value={field.sample}
        onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ sample: e.target.value })}
        placeholder="Sample value"
        className="data-field-sample"
      />
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Diagram Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * Draw.io integration modal
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface DiagramEditorProps {
  svg: string;
  xml: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (svg: string, xml: string) => void;
}

export const DiagramEditor: React.FC<DiagramEditorProps> = ({
  svg,
  xml,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  // Draw.io URL from env or default
  const DRAWIO_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_DRAWIO_URL || 'https://embed.diagrams.net/';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
      // Verify origin
      if (!event.origin.includes('diagrams.net') && !event.origin.includes('draw.io')) {
        return;
      }

      const msg = event.data;
      
      if (typeof msg === 'string') {
        // Handle init
        if (msg === 'ready') {
          setIsLoading(false);
          
          // Send initial XML if exists
          if (xml && iframeRef.current) {
            iframeRef.current.contentWindow?.postMessage(
              JSON.stringify({ action: 'load', xml }),
              '*'
            );
          }
        }
        
        // Handle save
        if (msg.startsWith('save:')) {
          const data = msg.slice(5);
          try {
            const { xml: savedXml, svg: savedSvg } = JSON.parse(data);
            onSave(savedSvg || '', savedXml);
          } catch {
            onSave('', data); // Fallback to xml only
          }
        }
        
        // Handle exit
        if (msg === 'exit') {
          onClose();
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
  }, [isOpen, xml, onSave, onClose]);

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (iframeRef.current) {
      iframeRef.current.contentWindow?.postMessage(
        JSON.stringify({ action: 'export', format: 'svg' }),
        '*'
      );
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="diagram-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="diagram-editor-modal">
        <div className="diagram-editor-header">
          <h3>Diagram Editor</h3>
          <div className="diagram-editor-actions">
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={handleSave}>
              рџ’ѕ Save
            </button>
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={onClose}>
              вњ• Close
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="diagram-editor-body">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="diagram-loading">
              <div className="diagram-spinner" />
              <p>Loading editor...</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <iframe
            ref={iframeRef}
            src={`${DRAWIO_URL}?embed=1&proto=json&spin=1`}
            className="diagram-iframe"
            sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-popups"
            allow="fullscreen"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

DiagramEditor.displayName = 'DiagramEditor';

/**
 * Main Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * Clean, focused component with single responsibility
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useSlides } from '../hooks/useSlides';
import { useSelection } from '../hooks/useSelection';
import { useHistory } from '../hooks/useHistory';
import { useFillMode } from '../hooks/useFillMode';
import { useAutoSave, loadAutoSave, clearAutoSave } from '../hooks/useAutoSave';
import { Canvas } from './Canvas';
import { LeftPanel } from './LeftPanel';
import { RightPanel } from './RightPanel';
import { SlidesBar } from './SlidesBar';
import { Toolbar } from './Toolbar';
import { ContextMenu } from './ContextMenu';
import { FloatingProps } from './FloatingProps';
import { EditorProvider } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export const Editor: React.FC = () => {
  const slides = useSlides();
  const selection = useSelection(slides.activeSlide?.elements ?? []);
  const history = useHistory();
  const pointerPosRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState<{ x: number; y: number; elementId: string | null } | null>(null);
  const [floatingPos, setFloatingPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [copiedStyle, setCopiedStyle] = useState<Partial<CanvasElement> | null>(null);

  // Auto-save
  useAutoSave(slides.slides, [], slides.activeSlideId);

  // Restore auto-save on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = loadAutoSave();
    if (saved && saved.slides.length > 0) {
      const when = new Date(saved.savedAt).toLocaleString();
      if (window.confirm(`Р’РѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ РЅРµСЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅРЅСѓСЋ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Сѓ РѕС‚ ${when}?`)) {
        // Restore slides
        // Note: This would need proper Slide entity reconstruction
      } else {
        clearAutoSave();
      }
    }
  }, []);

  // Fill mode hook
  const fillMode = useFillMode({
    slide: slides.activeSlide,
    addElement: slides.addElement,
    updateElement: slides.updateElement,
  });

  // Push to history when slides change
  useEffect(() => {
    history.push(slides.slides, slides.activeSlideId);
  }, [slides.slides, slides.activeSlideId]);

  const handleUndo = useCallback(() => {
    const entry = history.undo();
    if (entry) {
      // Restore slides from history
      slides.setSlides(entry.slides);
      slides.setActiveSlide(entry.activeSlideId ?? '');
    }
  }, [history, slides]);

  const handleRedo = useCallback(() => {
    const entry = history.redo();
    if (entry) {
      // Restore slides from history
      slides.setSlides(entry.slides);
      slides.setActiveSlide(entry.activeSlideId ?? '');
    }
  }, [history, slides]);

  // Import/Export handlers
  const handleImport = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json';
    input.onchange = (e) => {
      const file = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).files?.[0];
      if (file) {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (event) => {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(event.target?.result as string);
            if (data.slides) {
              // TODO: Validate and import slides
              console.log('Import data:', data);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Import failed:', err);
            alert('Failed to import file');
          }
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, []);

  const handleExportJSON = useCallback(() => {
    const data = {
      slides: slides.slides,
      activeSlideId: slides.activeSlideId,
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, [slides.slides, slides.activeSlideId]);

  const handleExportSVG = useCallback(() => {
    // TODO: Implement SVG export
    console.log('Export SVG');
    alert('SVG export not implemented yet');
  }, []);

  const handleExportPDF = useCallback(() => {
    // TODO: Implement PDF export
    console.log('Export PDF');
    alert('PDF export not implemented yet');
  }, []);

  const handleElementSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string, multi: boolean) => {
      selection.select(id, multi);
    },
    [selection]
  );

  const handleElementUpdate = useCallback(
    (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
      slides.updateElement(id, patch);
    },
    [slides]
  );

  const handleElementDelete = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      slides.removeElement(id);
      selection.clearSelection();
      setContextMenu(null);
      setFloatingPos(null);
    },
    [slides, selection]
  );

  const handleContextMenu = useCallback(
    (e: React.MouseEvent, elementId: string | null) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setContextMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, elementId });
      setFloatingPos(null);
    },
    []
  );

  const handleElementSelectWithPos = useCallback(
    (id: string, multi: boolean, clickPos?: { x: number; y: number }) => {
      selection.select(id, multi);
      if (id && clickPos) {
        setFloatingPos(clickPos);
      } else {
        setFloatingPos(null);
      }
    },
    [selection]
  );

  const handleCopyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    if (selection.selected) {
      const { id, type, x, y, width, height, rotation, ...style } = selection.selected;
      setCopiedStyle(style);
    }
  }, [selection.selected]);

  const handleApplyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    if (selection.selectedId && copiedStyle) {
      slides.updateElement(selection.selectedId, copiedStyle);
    }
  }, [selection.selectedId, copiedStyle, slides]);

  const handleMoveLayer = useCallback(
    (direction: 1 | -1) => {
      if (selection.selectedId && slides.activeSlide) {
        slides.moveElementLayer(selection.selectedId, direction);
      }
    },
    [selection.selectedId, slides]
  );

  // Keyboard shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Esc - cancel fill mode or clear selection
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (fillMode.isActive) {
          fillMode.cancel();
        } else {
          selection.clearSelection();
        }
      }
      // Delete - remove selected
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        if (selection.selectedId) {
          handleElementDelete(selection.selectedId);
        }
      }
      // Ctrl+Z - undo
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'z' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleUndo();
      }
      // Ctrl+Y or Ctrl+Shift+Z - redo
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === 'y' || (e.key === 'z' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleRedo();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [fillMode, selection, handleUndo, handleRedo, handleElementDelete]);

  if (!slides.activeSlide) {
    return <div className="editor-empty">No active slide</div>;
  }

  return (
    <EditorProvider
      slides={slides}
      selection={selection}
      history={history}
    >
      <div className="editor">
        <Toolbar
          onUndo={handleUndo}
          onRedo={handleRedo}
          canUndo={history.canUndo}
          canRedo={history.canRedo}
          onImport={handleImport}
          onExportJSON={handleExportJSON}
          onExportSVG={handleExportSVG}
          onExportPDF={handleExportPDF}
        />
        
        <div className="editor-body">
          <LeftPanel
            fillMode={{
              isActive: fillMode.isActive,
              activate: fillMode.activate,
              deactivate: fillMode.deactivate,
              setColor: fillMode.setColor,
              color: fillMode.color,
            }}
          />
          
          <main className="editor-canvas-area">
            <Canvas
              slide={slides.activeSlide}
              selectedIds={selection.selectedIds}
              onSelect={handleElementSelect}
              onUpdate={handleElementUpdate}
              onDelete={handleElementDelete}
              fillMode={{
                isActive: fillMode.isActive,
                points: fillMode.points,
                color: fillMode.color,
              }}
              onFillClick={() => {
                return fillMode.handleClick(pointerPosRef.current);
              }}
              onFillDoubleClick={() => {
                fillMode.closePolygon();
              }}
              onPointerMove={(pos) => {
                pointerPosRef.current = pos;
              }}
            />
          </main>
          
          <RightPanel
            selectedElements={selection.selectedElements}
            onUpdate={handleElementUpdate}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Context Menu */}
        {contextMenu && (
          <ContextMenu
            x={contextMenu.x}
            y={contextMenu.y}
            elementId={contextMenu.elementId}
            selectedElements={selection.selectedElements}
            onClose={() => setContextMenu(null)}
            onCut={() => { /* TODO */ }}
            onCopy={() => { /* TODO */ }}
            onPaste={() => { /* TODO */ }}
            onDuplicate={() => { /* TODO */ }}
            onDelete={() => handleElementDelete(selection.selectedId!)}
            onBringForward={() => handleMoveLayer(1)}
            onSendBackward={() => handleMoveLayer(-1)}
            onCopyStyle={handleCopyStyle}
            onApplyStyle={handleApplyStyle}
            canPaste={false}
            hasStyleCopied={!!copiedStyle}
          />
        )}

        {/* Floating Props */}
        {floatingPos && selection.selected && selection.selected.type !== 'text' && selection.selected.type !== 'footer' && (
          <FloatingProps
            x={floatingPos.x}
            y={floatingPos.y}
            element={selection.selected}
            onUpdate={(patch) => handleElementUpdate(selection.selected!.id, patch)}
            onClose={() => setFloatingPos(null)}
            onBringForward={() => handleMoveLayer(1)}
            onSendBackward={() => handleMoveLayer(-1)}
            onDelete={() => handleElementDelete(selection.selected!.id)}
          />
        )}
        
        <SlidesBar
          slides={slides.slides}
          activeId={slides.activeSlideId}
          onSelect={slides.setActiveSlide}
          onAdd={slides.addSlide}
          onRemove={slides.removeSlide}
          onDuplicate={slides.duplicateSlide}
        />
      </div>
    </EditorProvider>
  );
};

Editor.displayName = 'Editor';

/**
 * Element Renderer - Presentation Layer
 * Strategy pattern for rendering different element types
 */

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Rect, Circle, Text, Line, Image as KonvaImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import {
  isTextElement,
  isShapeElement,
  isImageElement,
  isLineElement,
  isTableElement,
  isFooterElement,
  isPlaceholderElement,
  isFillElement,
  isDiagramElement,
  isConnectorElement,
  isStampElement,
} from '../../shared/types';

interface ElementRendererProps {
  element: CanvasElement;
  isSelected: boolean;
  isHovered?: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onHover?: (hovered: boolean) => void;
  onDragStart?: () => void;
  onDragMove?: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  onDragEnd?: () => void;
}

export const ElementRenderer: React.FC<ElementRendererProps> = ({
  element,
  isSelected,
  isHovered,
  onSelect,
  onUpdate,
  onHover,
  onDragStart,
  onDragMove,
  onDragEnd,
}) => {
  const handleDragStart = React.useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
      onSelect();
      onDragStart?.();
    },
    [onSelect, onDragStart]
  );

  const handleDragMove = React.useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
      const node = e.target;
      onDragMove?.(node.x(), node.y());
    },
    [onDragMove]
  );

  const handleDragEnd = React.useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
      const node = e.target;
      onUpdate({
        x: node.x(),
        y: node.y(),
      });
      onDragEnd?.();
    },
    [onUpdate, onDragEnd]
  );

  const handleMouseEnter = React.useCallback(() => {
    onHover?.(true);
  }, [onHover]);

  const handleMouseLeave = React.useCallback(() => {
    onHover?.(false);
  }, [onHover]);

  const commonProps = useMemo(
    () => ({
      id: element.id,
      x: element.x,
      y: element.y,
      width: element.width,
      height: element.height,
      rotation: element.rotation,
      draggable: !element.locked,
      onDragStart: handleDragStart,
      onDragMove: handleDragMove,
      onDragEnd: handleDragEnd,
      onClick: onSelect,
      onTap: onSelect,
      onMouseEnter: handleMouseEnter,
      onMouseLeave: handleMouseLeave,
    }),
    [element, handleDragStart, handleDragMove, handleDragEnd, onSelect, handleMouseEnter, handleMouseLeave]
  );

  // Text Element
  if (isTextElement(element)) {
    return (
      <Rect {...commonProps} fill={element.background}>
        <Text
          text={element.text || 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚'}
          fontSize={element.fontSize}
          fontFamily={element.fontFamily}
          fill={element.fill}
          fontStyle={element.fontStyle}
          textDecoration={element.textDecoration}
          align={element.align}
          verticalAlign={element.verticalAlign}
          lineHeight={element.lineHeight}
          padding={element.padding.top}
          width={element.width - element.padding.left - element.padding.right}
          height={element.height - element.padding.top - element.padding.bottom}
        />
      </Rect>
    );
  }

  // Shape Element
  if (isShapeElement(element)) {
    if (element.shape === 'circle') {
      return (
        <Circle
          {...commonProps}
          radius={Math.min(element.width, element.height) / 2}
          fill={element.fill}
          stroke={element.stroke}
          strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
        />
      );
    }

    if (element.shape === 'roundRect') {
      return (
        <Rect
          {...commonProps}
          fill={element.fill}
          stroke={element.stroke}
          strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
          cornerRadius={element.radius ?? 20}
        />
      );
    }

    // Default rect
    return (
      <Rect
        {...commonProps}
        fill={element.fill}
        stroke={element.stroke}
        strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
      />
    );
  }

  // Image Element
  if (isImageElement(element)) {
    return <ImageElementRenderer element={element} commonProps={commonProps} />;
  }

  // Line Element
  if (isLineElement(element)) {
    return (
      <Line
        id={element.id}
        points={element.points ? [...element.points] : [0, 0, element.width, element.height]}
        stroke={element.stroke}
        strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
        dash={getDashArray(element.dash)}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        onClick={onSelect}
        onTap={onSelect}
      />
    );
  }

  // Table Element
  if (isTableElement(element)) {
    return <TableElementRenderer element={element} commonProps={commonProps} />;
  }

  // Footer Element
  if (isFooterElement(element)) {
    return (
      <Rect {...commonProps} fill={element.bgColor}>
        <Text
          text={element.text}
          fontSize={element.fontSize}
          fill={element.fill}
          align="center"
          verticalAlign="middle"
          width={element.width}
          height={element.height}
        />
      </Rect>
    );
  }

  // Placeholder Element
  if (isPlaceholderElement(element)) {
    return (
      <Rect {...commonProps} fill={element.background} stroke="#9ca3af" strokeWidth={1} dash={[4, 4]}>
        <Text
          text={element.label}
          fontSize={element.fontSize}
          fontFamily={element.fontFamily}
          fill="#9ca3af"
          align="center"
          verticalAlign="middle"
          width={element.width}
          height={element.height}
        />
      </Rect>
    );
  }

  // Fill Element
  if (isFillElement(element)) {
    return (
      <Rect
        {...commonProps}
        fill={element.fill}
        opacity={element.opacity}
        stroke={element.stroke}
        strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
      />
    );
  }

  // Diagram Element
  if (isDiagramElement(element)) {
    return <DiagramElementRenderer element={element} commonProps={commonProps} />;
  }

  // Connector Element
  if (isConnectorElement(element)) {
    return <ConnectorElementRenderer element={element} onSelect={onSelect} />;
  }

  // Stamp Element
  if (isStampElement(element)) {
    return <StampElementRenderer element={element} commonProps={commonProps} />;
  }

  // Unknown element type
  console.warn('Unknown element type:', element.type);
  return null;
};

ElementRenderer.displayName = 'ElementRenderer';

// Helper functions
function getDashArray(dash: string): number[] {
  switch (dash) {
    case 'dashed':
      return [10, 5];
    case 'dotted':
      return [2, 4];
    case 'dashdot':
      return [10, 5, 2, 5];
    case 'longdash':
      return [20, 5];
    default:
      return [];
  }
}

// Image Element Renderer
const ImageElementRenderer: React.FC<{
  element: import('../../shared/types').ImageElement;
  commonProps: Record<string, unknown>;
}> = ({ element, commonProps }) => {
  const [image, setImage] = React.useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const img = new window.Image();
    img.src = element.src;
    img.onload = () => setImage(img);
    img.onerror = () => console.error('Failed to load image:', element.src);
  }, [element.src]);

  if (!image) {
    return (
      <Rect
        {...commonProps}
        fill="#f3f4f6"
        stroke="#d1d5db"
        strokeWidth={1}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <KonvaImage
      {...commonProps}
      image={image}
    />
  );
};

// Table Element Renderer
const TableElementRenderer: React.FC<{
  element: import('../../shared/types').TableElement;
  commonProps: Record<string, unknown>;
}> = ({ element, commonProps }) => {
  const cellWidth = element.width / element.cols;
  const cellHeight = element.height / element.rows;

  return (
    <>
      <Rect {...commonProps} fill="#ffffff" stroke={element.borderColor} strokeWidth={1} />
      {element.data.map((row, rowIndex) =>
        row.map((cell, colIndex) => (
          <React.Fragment key={`${rowIndex}-${colIndex}`}>
            <Rect
              x={element.x + colIndex * cellWidth}
              y={element.y + rowIndex * cellHeight}
              width={cellWidth}
              height={cellHeight}
              fill={rowIndex === 0 ? element.headerBg : '#ffffff'}
              stroke={element.borderColor}
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
            <Text
              x={element.x + colIndex * cellWidth + 4}
              y={element.y + rowIndex * cellHeight + 4}
              width={cellWidth - 8}
              height={cellHeight - 8}
              text={cell}
              fontSize={element.fontSize}
              fill="#000000"
              align="left"
              verticalAlign="middle"
            />
          </React.Fragment>
        ))
      )}
    </>
  );
};

// Diagram Element Renderer
const DiagramElementRenderer: React.FC<{
  element: import('../../shared/types').DiagramElement;
  commonProps: Record<string, unknown>;
}> = ({ element, commonProps }) => {
  // Simplified SVG rendering
  return (
    <Rect
      {...commonProps}
      fill="#ffffff"
      stroke="#3b82f6"
      strokeWidth={2}
      dash={[5, 5]}
    />
  );
};

// Connector Element Renderer
const ConnectorElementRenderer: React.FC<{
  element: import('../../shared/types').ConnectorElement;
  onSelect: () => void;
}> = ({ element, onSelect }) => {
  // Simplified connector rendering
  return (
    <Line
      id={element.id}
      points={[0, 0, 100, 100]} // Calculate based on from/to elements
      stroke={element.stroke}
      strokeWidth={element.strokeWidth}
      dash={getDashArray(element.dash)}
      lineCap="round"
      lineJoin="round"
      onClick={onSelect}
      onTap={onSelect}
    />
  );
};

// Stamp Element Renderer
const StampElementRenderer: React.FC<{
  element: import('../../shared/types').StampElement;
  commonProps: Record<string, unknown>;
}> = ({ element, commonProps }) => {
  // Simplified stamp rendering
  return (
    <Rect
      {...commonProps}
      fill={element.stampColor ?? '#ef4444'}
      opacity={0.5}
    />
  );
};

/**
 * Floating Props Component - Presentation Layer
 * Quick properties panel near selection
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

interface FloatingPropsProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  element: CanvasElement;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

export const FloatingProps: React.FC<FloatingPropsProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  element,
  onUpdate,
  onClose,
  onBringForward,
  onSendBackward,
  onDelete,
}) => {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState({ x, y });

  // Clamp to viewport
  useEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;

    const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    const viewportWidth = window.innerWidth;
    const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;

    let newX = x;
    let newY = y - 50; // Show above element

    // Clamp X
    if (newX + rect.width > viewportWidth) {
      newX = viewportWidth - rect.width - 10;
    }
    if (newX < 10) {
      newX = 10;
    }

    // Clamp Y
    if (newY < 10) {
      newY = y + element.height + 10; // Show below if no space above
    }
    if (newY + rect.height > viewportHeight) {
      newY = viewportHeight - rect.height - 10;
    }

    setPosition({ x: newX, y: newY });
  }, [x, y, element.height]);

  // Close on click outside
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (panelRef.current && !panelRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    // Delay to avoid immediate close on open
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }, 100);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  // Render type-specific controls
  const renderTypeControls = () => {
    switch (element.type) {
      case 'shape':
        return (
          <>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Fill</label>
              <input
                type="color"
                value={element.fill}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Stroke</label>
              <input
                type="color"
                value={element.stroke}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
          </>
        );
      case 'text':
        return (
          <>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Color</label>
              <input
                type="color"
                value={element.fill}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Size</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={element.fontSize}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onUpdate({ fontSize: Number(e.target.value) })
                }
                min={8}
                max={72}
              />
            </div>
          </>
        );
      case 'line':
        return (
          <>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Color</label>
              <input
                type="color"
                value={element.stroke}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="fp-group">
              <label>Width</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={element.strokeWidth}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onUpdate({ strokeWidth: Number(e.target.value) })
                }
                min={1}
                max={10}
              />
            </div>
          </>
        );
      default:
        return null;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={panelRef}
      className="floating-props"
      style={{ left: position.x, top: position.y }}
    >
      {renderTypeControls()}

      <div className="fp-group">
        <button onClick={onBringForward} title="Bring Forward">
          в†‘
        </button>
        <button onClick={onSendBackward} title="Send Backward">
          в†“
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="fp-group">
        <button onClick={onDelete} className="danger" title="Delete">
          рџ—‘пёЏ
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FloatingProps.displayName = 'FloatingProps';

/**
 * Grid Layer Component - Presentation Layer
 * Renders canvas grid
 */

import React from 'react';
import { Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../shared/constants';

interface GridLayerProps {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export const GridLayer: React.FC<GridLayerProps> = ({ width, height }) => {
  return (
    <Shape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
        const context = ctx._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        context.beginPath();

        // Vertical lines
        for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(x, 0);
          context.lineTo(x, height);
        }

        // Horizontal lines
        for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(0, y);
          context.lineTo(width, y);
        }

        context.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
        context.lineWidth = 1;
        context.stroke();

        ctx.strokeShape(shape);
      }}
    />
  );
};

GridLayer.displayName = 'GridLayer';

/**
 * Inline Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * Contenteditable overlay for canvas text editing
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface InlineEditorProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  value: string;
  html?: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  verticalAlign: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  lineHeight: number;
  padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  background: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onChange: (value: { value?: string; html?: string }) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineEditor: React.FC<InlineEditorProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  width,
  height,
  value,
  html,
  fontSize,
  fontFamily,
  fontStyle,
  textDecoration,
  fill,
  align,
  verticalAlign,
  lineHeight,
  padding,
  background,
  isOpen,
  onChange,
  onCommit,
  onCancel,
}) => {
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isComposing, setIsComposing] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !editorRef.current) return;

    const editor = editorRef.current;
    editor.innerHTML = html || value || '<p><br></p>';
    editor.focus();

    // Select all content
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(editor);
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection) {
      selection.removeAllRanges();
      selection.addRange(range);
    }
  }, [isOpen, html, value]);

  const handleInput = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editorRef.current) return;
    const newHtml = editorRef.current.innerHTML;
    const newText = editorRef.current.innerText;
    onChange({ value: newText, html: newHtml });
  }, [onChange]);

  const handleKeyDown = useCallback(
    (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (isComposing) return;

      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        onCommit();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onCancel();
      }
    },
    [isComposing, onCommit, onCancel]
  );

  const handleBlur = useCallback(() => {
    // Small delay to allow click events to process
    setTimeout(() => {
      onCommit();
    }, 100);
  }, [onCommit]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const verticalAlignMap = {
    top: 'flex-start',
    middle: 'center',
    bottom: 'flex-end',
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="inline-editor-overlay"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        left: x,
        top: y,
        width,
        height,
        zIndex: 1000,
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={editorRef}
        className="inline-editor"
        contentEditable
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={handleInput}
        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        onCompositionStart={() => setIsComposing(true)}
        onCompositionEnd={() => setIsComposing(false)}
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          padding: `${padding.top}px ${padding.right}px ${padding.bottom}px ${padding.left}px`,
          fontSize: `${fontSize}px`,
          fontFamily,
          fontStyle,
          textDecoration,
          color: fill,
          backgroundColor: background === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' : background,
          textAlign: align,
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          justifyContent: verticalAlignMap[verticalAlign],
          lineHeight,
          outline: 'none',
          border: '2px solid #3b82f6',
          borderRadius: '4px',
          boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)',
          overflow: 'auto',
          wordWrap: 'break-word',
          whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

InlineEditor.displayName = 'InlineEditor';

/**
 * Left Panel Component - Presentation Layer
 * Tools and element creation
 */

import React, { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useSlidesContext } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import type { CanvasElement, BasicShape } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface LeftPanelProps {
  fillMode?: {
    isActive: boolean;
    activate: () => void;
    deactivate: () => void;
    setColor: (color: string) => void;
    color: string;
  };
}

export const LeftPanel: React.FC<LeftPanelProps> = ({ fillMode }) => {
  const { addElement, activeSlide } = useSlidesContext();

  const handleAddText = useCallback(() => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    const element: CanvasElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'text',
      x: 100,
      y: 100,
      width: 200,
      height: 50,
      rotation: 0,
      text: '',
      richText: true,
      fontSize: 16,
      fontFamily: 'Arial',
      fill: '#000000',
      fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: 'none',
      align: 'left',
      verticalAlign: 'top',
      lineHeight: 1.2,
      padding: { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
      background: 'transparent',
    };
    addElement(element);
  }, [addElement, activeSlide]);

  const handleAddShape = useCallback((shape: BasicShape) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    const element: CanvasElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'shape',
      x: 100,
      y: 100,
      width: 100,
      height: 100,
      rotation: 0,
      shape,
      fill: '#3b82f6',
      stroke: '#1d4ed8',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      text: '',
      fontSize: 14,
      textFill: '#ffffff',
    };
    addElement(element);
  }, [addElement, activeSlide]);

  const handleAddImage = useCallback(() => {
    // Trigger file input
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = (e) => {
      const file = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (event) => {
        if (!activeSlide) return;
        const element: CanvasElement = {
          id: generateId(),
          type: 'image',
          x: 100,
          y: 100,
          width: 200,
          height: 200,
          rotation: 0,
          src: event.target?.result as string,
        };
        addElement(element);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [addElement, activeSlide]);

  return (
    <aside className="left-panel">
      <div className="panel-section">
        <h3>Tools</h3>
        <button onClick={handleAddText} className="tool-button">
          рџ“ќ Text
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => handleAddShape('rect')} className="tool-button">
          в¬њ Rectangle
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => handleAddShape('circle')} className="tool-button">
          в­• Circle
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => handleAddShape('roundRect')} className="tool-button">
          рџ”І Rounded Rect
        </button>
        <button onClick={handleAddImage} className="tool-button">
          рџ–јпёЏ Image
        </button>
        <div className="tool-divider" />
        <button
          onClick={fillMode?.isActive ? fillMode.deactivate : fillMode?.activate}
          className={`tool-button ${fillMode?.isActive ? 'active' : ''}`}
        >
          рџЋЁ Fill Mode {fillMode?.isActive ? '(ON)' : ''}
        </button>
        {fillMode?.isActive && (
          <div className="fill-color-picker">
            <label>Fill Color:</label>
            <input
              type="color"
              value={fillMode.color}
              onChange={(e) => fillMode.setColor(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

LeftPanel.displayName = 'LeftPanel';

FILE: presentation\components\RichTextEditor.tsx

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Rich Text Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * TipTap integration for text editing
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import Underline from '@tiptap/extension-underline';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import TextStyle from '@tiptap/extension-text-style';
import Color from '@tiptap/extension-color';
import Highlight from '@tiptap/extension-highlight';
import Link from '@tiptap/extension-link';
import Table from '@tiptap/extension-table';
import TableRow from '@tiptap/extension-table-row';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';
import FontFamily from '@tiptap/extension-font-family';

interface RichTextEditorProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  initialHtml: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (html: string) => void;
}

export const RichTextEditor: React.FC<RichTextEditorProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  initialHtml,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const [showLinkInput, setShowLinkInput] = useState(false);
  const [linkUrl, setLinkUrl] = useState('');
  const linkInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit,
      Underline,
      TextAlign.configure({
        types: ['heading', 'paragraph'],
      }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
      Link.configure({
        openOnClick: false,
      }),
      Table.configure({
        resizable: true,
      }),
      TableRow,
      TableCell,
      TableHeader,
      FontFamily,
    ],
    content: initialHtml || '<p></p>',
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && editor) {
      editor.commands.setContent(initialHtml || '<p></p>');
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialHtml, editor]);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    if (editor) {
      onSave(editor.getHTML());
    }
    onClose();
  }, [editor, onSave, onClose]);

  const handleSetLink = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    
    if (showLinkInput) {
      if (linkUrl) {
        editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange('link').setLink({ href: linkUrl }).run();
      } else {
        editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange('link').unsetLink().run();
      }
      setShowLinkInput(false);
      setLinkUrl('');
    } else {
      const previousUrl = editor.getAttributes('link').href;
      setLinkUrl(previousUrl || '');
      setShowLinkInput(true);
      setTimeout(() => linkInputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
    }
  }, [editor, showLinkInput, linkUrl]);

  const addTable = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: 3, cols: 3, withHeaderRow: true }).run();
  }, [editor]);

  const toggleHeading = useCallback((level: 1 | 2 | 3) => {
    if (!editor) return;
    editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level }).run();
  }, [editor]);

  if (!isOpen || !editor) return null;

  return (
    <div className="rt-modal-backdrop">
      <div className="rt-modal">
        <div className="rt-header">
          <h3>Rich Text Editor</h3>
          <button className="rt-close" onClick={onClose}>Г—</button>
        </div>

        <div className="rt-toolbar">
          {/* Text Style */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('bold') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Bold (Ctrl+B)"
            >
              <b>B</b>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('italic') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Italic (Ctrl+I)"
            >
              <i>I</i>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('underline') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Underline (Ctrl+U)"
            >
              <u>U</u>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('strike') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Strikethrough"
            >
              <s>S</s>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Headings */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button
              onClick={() => toggleHeading(1)}
              className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Heading 1"
            >
              H1
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => toggleHeading(2)}
              className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Heading 2"
            >
              H2
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => toggleHeading(3)}
              className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Heading 3"
            >
              H3
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Alignment */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}
              className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Align Left"
            >
              в¬…пёЏ
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}
              className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Align Center"
            >
              в†”пёЏ
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}
              className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' }) ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Align Right"
            >
              вћЎпёЏ
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Colors */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <input
              type="color"
              value={editor.getAttributes('textStyle').color || '#000000'}
              onChange={(e) => editor.chain().focus().setColor(e.target.value).run()}
              title="Text Color"
            />
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHighlight().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('highlight') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Highlight"
            >
              рџ–ЌпёЏ
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Lists */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('bulletList') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Bullet List"
            >
              вЂў List
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}
              className={editor.isActive('orderedList') ? 'active' : ''}
              title="Ordered List"
            >
              1. List
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Link */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            {showLinkInput ? (
              <>
                <input
                  ref={linkInputRef}
                  type="text"
                  value={linkUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setLinkUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://..."
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSetLink()}
                />
                <button onClick={handleSetLink}>вњ“</button>
                <button onClick={() => setShowLinkInput(false)}>Г—</button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <button
                onClick={handleSetLink}
                className={editor.isActive('link') ? 'active' : ''}
                title="Add Link"
              >
                рџ”—
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Table */}
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button onClick={addTable} title="Insert Table">
              вЉћ Table
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addColumnBefore().run()}
              disabled={!editor.can().addColumnBefore()}
              title="Add Column Before"
            >
              +Col
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addRowBefore().run()}
              disabled={!editor.can().addRowBefore()}
              title="Add Row Before"
            >
              +Row
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rt-content-wrapper">
          <EditorContent editor={editor} />
        </div>

        <div className="rt-footer">
          <button className="rt-btn" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="rt-btn primary" onClick={handleSave}>Save</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

RichTextEditor.displayName = 'RichTextEditor';

/**
 * Right Panel Component - Presentation Layer
 * Properties editor for selected elements with Data/Properties tabs
 */

import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { DataPanel } from './DataPanel';

interface RightPanelProps {
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  onUpdate: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
}

type Tab = 'data' | 'properties';

export const RightPanel: React.FC<RightPanelProps> = ({
  selectedElements,
  onUpdate,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>('properties');
  
  const selectedElement = useMemo(
    () => (selectedElements.length === 1 ? selectedElements[0] : null),
    [selectedElements]
  );

  const handleChange = useCallback(
    (field: string, value: unknown) => {
      if (!selectedElement) return;
      onUpdate(selectedElement.id, { [field]: value });
    },
    [selectedElement, onUpdate]
  );

  return (
    <aside className="right-panel">
      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="rp-tabs">
        <button
          className={`rp-tab ${activeTab === 'data' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('data')}
        >
          рџ“Љ Data
        </button>
        <button
          className={`rp-tab ${activeTab === 'properties' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('properties')}
        >
          вљ™пёЏ Properties
          {selectedElements.length > 0 && (
            <span className="rp-tab-badge">{selectedElements.length}</span>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Tab Content */}
      <div className="rp-body">
        {activeTab === 'data' && <DataPanel />}
        
        {activeTab === 'properties' && (
          <PropertiesTab
            selectedElements={selectedElements}
            selectedElement={selectedElement}
            onUpdate={onUpdate}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

interface PropertiesTabProps {
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  selectedElement: CanvasElement | null;
  onUpdate: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
}

const PropertiesTab: React.FC<PropertiesTabProps> = ({
  selectedElements,
  selectedElement,
  onUpdate,
}) => {
  const handleChange = useCallback(
    (field: string, value: unknown) => {
      if (!selectedElement) return;
      onUpdate(selectedElement.id, { [field]: value });
    },
    [selectedElement, onUpdate]
  );

  if (selectedElements.length === 0) {
    return <div className="panel-empty">Select an element to edit properties</div>;
  }

  if (selectedElements.length > 1) {
    return (
      <div className="panel-header">
        <h3>{selectedElements.length} elements selected</h3>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!selectedElement) return null;

  return (
    <div className="panel-content">
      <div className="panel-header">
        <h3>Properties</h3>
        <span className="element-type">{selectedElement.type}</span>
      </div>
        {/* Position */}
        <div className="property-group">
          <h4>Position</h4>
          <div className="property-row">
            <label>X:</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(selectedElement.x)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange('x', Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="property-row">
            <label>Y:</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(selectedElement.y)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange('y', Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Size */}
        <div className="property-group">
          <h4>Size</h4>
          <div className="property-row">
            <label>Width:</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(selectedElement.width)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange('width', Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="property-row">
            <label>Height:</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(selectedElement.height)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange('height', Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Rotation */}
        <div className="property-group">
          <h4>Rotation</h4>
          <div className="property-row">
            <label>Angle:</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(selectedElement.rotation)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange('rotation', Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Type-specific properties */}
        {selectedElement.type === 'text' && (
          <TextProperties element={selectedElement} onChange={handleChange} />
        )}

        {selectedElement.type === 'shape' && (
          <ShapeProperties element={selectedElement} onChange={handleChange} />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

RightPanel.displayName = 'RightPanel';

// Text Properties
const TextProperties: React.FC<{

          />

            value={element.fontFamily}
            onChange={(e) => onChange('fontFamily', e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="Arial">Arial</option>
            <option value="Times New Roman">Times New Roman</option>
            <option value="Helvetica">Helvetica</option>
            <option value="Georgia">Georgia</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="property-row">
          <label>Color:</label>
          <input
            type="color"
            value={element.fill}
            onChange={(e) => onChange('fill', e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

// Shape Properties
