  element: import('../../shared/types').ShapeElement;
  onChange: (field: string, value: unknown) => void;
}> = ({ element, onChange }) => {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="property-group">
        <h4>Appearance</h4>
        <div className="property-row">
          <label>Fill:</label>
          <input
            type="color"
            value={element.fill}
            onChange={(e) => onChange('fill', e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="property-row">
          <label>Stroke:</label>
          <input
            type="color"
            value={element.stroke}
            onChange={(e) => onChange('stroke', e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="property-row">
          <label>Stroke Width:</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={element.strokeWidth}
            onChange={(e) => onChange('strokeWidth', Number(e.target.value))}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

/**
 * Slides Bar Component - Presentation Layer
 * Slide navigation and management
 */

import React, { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';

interface SlidesBarProps {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeId: string | null;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  onAdd: () => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
  onDuplicate: (id: string) => Slide | null;
}

export const SlidesBar: React.FC<SlidesBarProps> = ({
  slides,
  activeId,
  onSelect,
  onAdd,
  onRemove,
  onDuplicate,
}) => {
  const handleDuplicate = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      const clone = onDuplicate(id);
      if (clone) {
        onSelect(clone.id);
      }
    },
    [onDuplicate, onSelect]
  );

  return (
    <div className="slides-bar">
      <div className="slides-header">
        <h3>Slides</h3>
        <button onClick={onAdd} className="btn-add">
          + Add
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="slides-list">
        {slides.map((slide, index) => (
          <SlideThumb
            key={slide.id}
            slide={slide}
            index={index}
            isActive={slide.id === activeId}
            onSelect={() => onSelect(slide.id)}
            onRemove={() => onRemove(slide.id)}
            onDuplicate={() => handleDuplicate(slide.id)}
            canRemove={slides.length > 1}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

SlidesBar.displayName = 'SlidesBar';

interface SlideThumbProps {
  slide: Slide;
  index: number;
  isActive: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  canRemove: boolean;
}

const SlideThumb: React.FC<SlideThumbProps> = ({
  slide,
  index,
  isActive,
  onSelect,
  onRemove,
  onDuplicate,
  canRemove,
}) => {
  const scale = 0.15;

  return (
    <div
      className={`slide-thumb ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}`}
      onClick={onSelect}
    >
      <div className="slide-number">{index + 1}</div>
      <div
        className="slide-preview"
        style={{
          width: slide.width * scale,
          height: slide.height * scale,
          backgroundColor: slide.background,
        }}
      >
        {/* Simplified preview */}
        {slide.elements.slice(0, 5).map((el) => (
          <div
            key={el.id}
            className="slide-preview-element"
            style={{
              left: el.x * scale,
              top: el.y * scale,
              width: el.width * scale,
              height: el.height * scale,
              backgroundColor: el.type === 'shape' ? (el as any).fill : '#ccc',
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="slide-actions">
        <button onClick={onDuplicate} title="Duplicate">
          рџ“‹
        </button>
        {canRemove && (
          <button onClick={onRemove} title="Remove">
            рџ—‘пёЏ
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Stamp Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * Modal editor for stamp elements
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Circle, Text, Image as KonvaImage, Line, Transformer } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';
import { useStampFillMode } from '../hooks/useStampFillMode';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './stamps/CompositeFillLayer';

interface StampEditorProps {
  stamp: StampElement;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (stamp: StampElement) => void;
}

export const StampEditor: React.FC<StampEditorProps> = ({
  stamp,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const [shapes, setShapes] = useState<StampShapeItem[]>(stamp.shapes ?? []);
  const [texts, setTexts] = useState<StampTextItem[]>(stamp.texts ?? []);
  const [images, setImages] = useState<StampImageItem[]>(stamp.images ?? []);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);

  const stageRef = React.useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = React.useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);

  // Reset state when opening
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setShapes(stamp.shapes ?? []);
      setTexts(stamp.texts ?? []);
      setImages(stamp.images ?? []);
      setSelectedId(null);
    }
  }, [isOpen, stamp]);

  const handleAddShape = useCallback((kind: StampShapeItem['kind']) => {
    const newShape: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      kind,
      x: stamp.width / 2 - 50,
      y: stamp.height / 2 - 50,
      width: 100,
      height: 100,
      cornerRadius: kind === 'rect' ? 8 : 0,
      stroke: '#000000',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      dash: 'solid',
    };
    setShapes((prev) => [...prev, newShape]);
    setSelectedId(newShape.id);
  }, [stamp.width, stamp.height]);

  const handleAddText = useCallback(() => {
    const newText: StampTextItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      text: 'New Text',
      mode: 'straight',
      x: stamp.width / 2 - 50,
      y: stamp.height / 2 - 10,
      width: 100,
      align: 'center',
      arcCx: stamp.width / 2,
      arcCy: stamp.height / 2,
      arcRadius: 50,
      arcStartDeg: 0,
      arcEndDeg: 180,
      arcFlip: false,
      fontSize: 14,
      fontFamily: 'Arial',
      fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: 'none',
      fill: '#000000',
    };
    setTexts((prev) => [...prev, newText]);
    setSelectedId(newText.id);
  }, [stamp.width, stamp.height]);

  const handleUpdateItem = useCallback((id: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    setShapes((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s))
    );
    setTexts((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...patch } : t))
    );
    setImages((prev) =>
      prev.map((i) => (i.id === id ? { ...i, ...patch } : i))
    );
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setShapes((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
    setTexts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
    setImages((prev) => prev.filter((i) => i.id !== id));
    setSelectedId(null);
  }, []);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    onSave({
      ...stamp,
      shapes,
      texts,
      images,
    });
    onClose();
  }, [stamp, shapes, texts, images, onSave, onClose]);

  // Fill mode hook
  const fillModeApi = useStampFillMode({
    shapes,
    texts,
    images,
    width: stamp.width,
    height: stamp.height,
    commit: (newShapes, newTexts, newImages) => {
      setShapes(newShapes);
      setTexts(newTexts);
      setImages(newImages);
    },
    select: (id) => setSelectedId(id),
  });

  // Handle canvas click for fill mode
  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
    if (fillModeApi.fillMode) {
      const pos = e.target.getStage()?.getPointerPosition();
      if (pos) {
        fillModeApi.handleCanvasClick(pos.x / zoom, pos.y / zoom);
      }
      return;
    }
    // Normal click handling
    if (e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
      setSelectedId(null);
    }
  }, [fillModeApi, zoom]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="stamp-editor-head">
          <input
            type="text"
            className="stamp-editor-name"
            placeholder="Stamp Name"
            defaultValue="Custom Stamp"
          />
          <div className="stamp-editor-head-right">
            <label>
              W:
              <input type="number" value={stamp.width} readOnly />
            </label>
            <label>
              H:
              <input type="number" value={stamp.height} readOnly />

interface StampItemPropertiesProps {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  selectedId: string;
  onUpdate: (id: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
}

const StampItemProperties: React.FC<StampItemPropertiesProps> = ({
  shapes,
  texts,
  selectedId,
  onUpdate,
}) => {
  const shape = shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId);
  const text = texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId);

  if (shape) {
    return (
      <>
        <h4>Shape Properties</h4>
        <label>Stroke</label>
        <input
          type="color"
          value={shape.stroke}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(shape.id, { stroke: e.target.value })}
        />
        <label>Stroke Width</label>
        <input
          type="number"
          value={shape.strokeWidth}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(shape.id, { strokeWidth: Number(e.target.value) })}
        />
        <label>Fill</label>
        <input
          type="color"
          value={shape.fill || '#ffffff'}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(shape.id, { fill: e.target.value })}
        />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (text) {
    return (
      <>
        <h4>Text Properties</h4>
        <label>Text</label>
        <textarea
          value={text.text}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(text.id, { text: e.target.value })}
          rows={3}
        />
        <label>Font Size</label>
        <input
          type="number"
          value={text.fontSize}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(text.id, { fontSize: Number(e.target.value) })}
        />
        <label>Color</label>
        <input
          type="color"
          value={text.fill}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdate(text.id, { fill: e.target.value })}
        />
      </>
    );
  }

  return null;
};

FILE: presentation\components\stamps\CompositeFillLayer.tsx
SIZE: 3270 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import { useFillWorker } from '../../hooks/useFillWorker';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function anchorToWorld(
  target: StampShapeItem,
  local: { x: number; y: number },
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const lx = target.x + local.x * target.width;
  const ly = target.y + local.y * target.height;
  const cx = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const cy = target.y + target.height / 2;
  const rot = ((target.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dx = lx - cx;
  const dy = ly - cy;
  return {
    x: cx + dx * Math.cos(rot) - dy * Math.sin(rot),
    y: cy + dx * Math.sin(rot) + dy * Math.cos(rot),
  };
}

export const CompositeFillLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width,
  height,
  shapes,
  enabled = true,
}) => {
  const { result, rasterize } = useFillWorker();
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const debounceRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const fills: FillSpec[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FillSpec[] = [];
    for (const s of shapes) {
      if (s.kind !== 'fill') continue;
      if (s.hidden) continue;
      if (!s.fillTargetShapeId) continue;

      const target = shapes.find((t) => t.id === s.fillTargetShapeId);
      if (!target) continue;

      const local = s.fillAnchorLocal ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };
      const { x, y } = anchorToWorld(target, local);

      out.push({
        px: x,
        py: y,
        color: s.fill || '#fbbf24',
        target,
      });
    }
    return out;
  }, [shapes]);

  const signature = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = shapes
      .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
      .map(
        (s) =>
          `${s.kind}:${Math.round(s.x)}|${Math.round(s.y)}|${Math.round(s.width)}|${Math.round(s.height)}|${Math.round(s.rotation || 0)}|${(s.points || []).map((v) => Math.round(v)).join(',')}|${s.strokeWidth}`,
      )
      .join('||');
    const f = fills
      .map((x) => `${Math.round(x.px)},${Math.round(x.py)},${x.color},${x.target?.id ?? '-'}`)
      .join(';');
    return geo + '###' + f;
  }, [shapes, fills]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !fills.length) {
      setImg(null);
      return;
    }
    if (debounceRef.current) window.clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);

    debounceRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      rasterize(signature, width, height, shapes, fills);
    }, 80);

    return () => {
      if (debounceRef.current) window.clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    };
  }, [signature, width, height, enabled, fills, shapes, rasterize]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!result || result.key !== signature) return;
    if (!result.url) { setImg(null); return; }
    const next = new window.Image();
    next.onload = () => setImg(next);
    next.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    next.src = result.url;
  }, [result, signature]);

  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      image={img}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

interface ToolbarProps {
  onUndo: () => void;
  onRedo: () => void;

  canRedo: boolean;

  onExportJSON: () => void;

  onExportPDF: () => void;

  onImport,
  onExportJSON,
  onExportSVG,
  onExportPDF,

  const handleExportClick = useCallback(() => {
    setShowExportMenu((prev) => !prev);

    setShowExportMenu(false);
    if (type === 'json') onExportJSON();

      <div className="toolbar-left">
        <h1>Ticket Editor</h1>

      <div className="toolbar-center">

          onClick={onUndo}

        <button
          onClick={onRedo}
          disabled={!canRedo}

          в†ЄпёЏ Redo

/**
 * Zoom Bar Component - Presentation Layer
 * Detailed zoom control
 */

import React, { useCallback } from 'react';

interface ZoomBarProps {
  zoom: number;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
  step?: number;
  onZoomChange: (zoom: number) => void;
}

export const ZoomBar: React.FC<ZoomBarProps> = ({
  zoom,
  minZoom = 0.2,
  maxZoom = 2.0,
  step = 0.1,
  onZoomChange,
}) => {
  const handleZoomIn = useCallback(() => {
    const newZoom = Math.min(maxZoom, Math.round((zoom + step) * 10) / 10);
    onZoomChange(newZoom);
  }, [zoom, maxZoom, step, onZoomChange]);

  const handleZoomOut = useCallback(() => {
    const newZoom = Math.max(minZoom, Math.round((zoom - step) * 10) / 10);
    onZoomChange(newZoom);
  }, [zoom, minZoom, step, onZoomChange]);

  const handleReset = useCallback(() => {
    onZoomChange(1);
  }, [onZoomChange]);

  const handleInputChange = useCallback(
    (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
      const value = parseFloat(e.target.value) / 100;
      if (!isNaN(value)) {
        const clamped = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, value));
        onZoomChange(Math.round(clamped * 10) / 10);
      }
    },
    [minZoom, maxZoom, onZoomChange]
  );

  const displayPercent = Math.round(zoom * 100);

  return (
    <div className="zoom-bar">
      <button
        className="zoom-btn"
        onClick={handleZoomOut}
        disabled={zoom <= minZoom}
        title="Zoom Out"
      >
        в€’
      </button>

      <div className="zoom-slider-wrapper">
        <input
          type="range"
          min={minZoom * 100}
          max={maxZoom * 100}
          step={step * 100}
          value={displayPercent}
          onChange={handleInputChange}
          className="zoom-slider"
        />
      </div>

      <button
        className="zoom-btn"
        onClick={handleZoomIn}
        disabled={zoom >= maxZoom}
        title="Zoom In"
      >
        +
      </button>

      <div className="zoom-display">
        <input
          type="number"
          value={displayPercent}
          onChange={handleInputChange}
          min={minZoom * 100}
          max={maxZoom * 100}
          className="zoom-input"
        />
        <span className="zoom-percent">%</span>
      </div>

      <button
        className="zoom-btn zoom-reset"
        onClick={handleReset}
        disabled={zoom === 1}
        title="Reset Zoom (100%)"
      >
        вџІ
      </button>

      <div className="zoom-presets">
        {[25, 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, 200].map((preset) => (
          <button
            key={preset}
            className={`zoom-preset ${displayPercent === preset ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => onZoomChange(preset / 100)}
          >
            {preset}%
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

ZoomBar.displayName = 'ZoomBar';

/**
 * Anchor Overlay Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle anchor points for connector creation
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export interface AnchorPoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  position: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  elementId: string;
}

interface UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  hoveredElementId: string | null;
  anchors: AnchorPoint[];
  showAnchors: (elementId: string, anchors: AnchorPoint[]) => void;
  hideAnchors: () => void;
  setHoveredElement: (id: string | null) => void;
}

export function useAnchorOverlay(): UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  const [hoveredElementId, setHoveredElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [anchors, setAnchors] = useState<AnchorPoint[]>([]);

  const showAnchors = useCallback((elementId: string, newAnchors: AnchorPoint[]) => {
    setHoveredElementId(elementId);
    setAnchors(newAnchors);
  }, []);

  const hideAnchors = useCallback(() => {
    setHoveredElementId(null);
    setAnchors([]);
  }, []);

  const setHoveredElement = useCallback((id: string | null) => {
    setHoveredElementId(id);
    if (!id) {
      setAnchors([]);
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    hoveredElementId,
    anchors,
    showAnchors,
    hideAnchors,
    setHoveredElement,
  };
}

/**
 * Auto Save Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Automatic saving to localStorage
 */

import { useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

const AUTO_SAVE_KEY = 'ticket-editor:autosave';
const AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 30000; // 30 seconds
const MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours

interface AutoSaveData {
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

export function useAutoSave(
  slides: Slide[],
  data: DataRoot[],
  activeSlideId: string | null
): void {
  // Auto save every 30 seconds
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const saveData: AutoSaveData = {
        slides,
        data,
        activeSlideId,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(saveData));
    }, AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [slides, data, activeSlideId]);
}

export function loadAutoSave(): AutoSaveData | null {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return null;

    const data: AutoSaveData = JSON.parse(saved);
    const savedAt = new Date(data.savedAt);
    const now = new Date();

    // Check if older than 24 hours
    if (now.getTime() - savedAt.getTime() > MAX_AGE_MS) {
      clearAutoSave();
      return null;
    }

    return data;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAutoSave(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
}

export function hasAutoSave(): boolean {
  return localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY) !== null;
}

/**
 * Connectors Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle connector creation and management
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement, ConnectorElement, AnchorSide } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

export interface ConnectorDraft {
  fromId: string;
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
  hoverTargetId?: string;
  hoverAnchor?: AnchorSide;
}

interface UseConnectorsOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  removeElement: (id: string) => void;
}

export interface UseConnectorsReturn {
  draft: ConnectorDraft | null;
  startDraft: (fromId: string, fromAnchor: AnchorSide, x: number, y: number) => void;
  moveDraft: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  commitDraft: () => void;
  cancelDraft: () => void;
  findNearestAnchor: (x: number, y: number) => { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null;
}

const ANCHOR_OFFSET = 8;

export function useConnectors(options: UseConnectorsOptions): UseConnectorsReturn {
  const { slide, addElement } = options;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ConnectorDraft | null>(null);

  const getAnchorPosition = useCallback((
    element: CanvasElement,
    anchor: AnchorSide
  ): { x: number; y: number } => {
    const cx = element.x + element.width / 2;
    const cy = element.y + element.height / 2;

    switch (anchor) {
      case 'top':
        return { x: cx, y: element.y - ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'right':
        return { x: element.x + element.width + ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
      case 'bottom':
        return { x: cx, y: element.y + element.height + ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'left':
        return { x: element.x - ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
    }
  }, []);

  const findNearestAnchor = useCallback((
    x: number,
    y: number
  ): { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null => {
    if (!slide) return null;

    let nearest: { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide; dist: number } | null = null;

    for (const el of slide.elements) {
      // Skip connectors and fills
      if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill') continue;

      const anchors: AnchorSide[] = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];
      
      for (const anchor of anchors) {
        const pos = getAnchorPosition(el, anchor);
        const dist = Math.hypot(pos.x - x, pos.y - y);
        
        if (dist < 30 && (!nearest || dist < nearest.dist)) {
          nearest = { elementId: el.id, anchor, dist };
        }
      }
    }

    return nearest ? { elementId: nearest.elementId, anchor: nearest.anchor } : null;
  }, [slide, getAnchorPosition]);

  const startDraft = useCallback((
    fromId: string,
    fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
    x: number,
    y: number
  ) => {
    setDraft({
      fromId,
      fromAnchor,
      toX: x,
      toY: y,
    });
  }, []);

  const moveDraft = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!draft) return;

    const nearest = findNearestAnchor(x, y);
    
    setDraft({
      ...draft,
      toX: x,
      toY: y,
      hoverTargetId: nearest?.elementId,
      hoverAnchor: nearest?.anchor,
    });
  }, [draft, findNearestAnchor]);

  const commitDraft = useCallback(() => {
    if (!draft || !slide) return;

    // Don't create if no target
    if (!draft.hoverTargetId || !draft.hoverAnchor) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    // Don't connect to self
    if (draft.fromId === draft.hoverTargetId) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    const connector: ConnectorElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'connector',
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      rotation: 0,
      fromId: draft.fromId,
      fromAnchor: draft.fromAnchor,
      toId: draft.hoverTargetId,
      toAnchor: draft.hoverAnchor,
      stroke: '#374151',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      dash: 'solid',
      isArrow: true,
      routing: 'straight',
    };

    addElement(connector);
    setDraft(null);
  }, [draft, slide, addElement]);

  const cancelDraft = useCallback(() => {
    setDraft(null);
  }, []);

  return {
    draft,
    startDraft,
    moveDraft,
    commitDraft,
    cancelDraft,
    findNearestAnchor,
  };
}

/**
 * Fill Mode Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handles fill polygon creation and shape filling
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement, FillElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface UseFillModeOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  updateElement: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
}

export interface UseFillModeReturn {
  isActive: boolean;
  color: string;
  points: readonly number[];
  activate: () => void;
  deactivate: () => void;
  setColor: (color: string) => void;
  handleClick: (pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }) => boolean;
  closePolygon: () => void;
  cancel: () => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

/**
 * Check if point hits a shape element
 */
function hitShapeElement(el: CanvasElement, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  // Shape element hit test
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    if (px < el.x || py < el.y || px > el.x + el.width || py > el.y + el.height) {
      return false;
    }
    if (el.shape === 'circle') {
      const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
      const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
      const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
      return Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) <= r;
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Fill element hit test (point-in-polygon)
  if (el.type === 'fill' && el.points && el.points.length >= 6) {
    const pts = el.points;
    const n = pts.length / 2;
    let inside = false;

    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      const xi = el.x + pts[i * 2];
      const yi = el.y + pts[i * 2 + 1];
      const xj = el.x + pts[j * 2];
      const yj = el.y + pts[j * 2 + 1];

      const intersect =
        yi > py !== yj > py &&
        px < ((xj - xi) * (py - yi)) / (yj - yi + 0.000001) + xi;

      if (intersect) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Find shape element at position
 */
function findShapeAt(
  slide: Slide | null,
  x: number,
  y: number
): CanvasElement | null {
  if (!slide) return null;
  
  for (let i = slide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = slide.elements[i];
    if (hitShapeElement(el, x, y)) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function useFillMode(options: UseFillModeOptions): UseFillModeReturn {
  const { slide, addElement, updateElement } = options;

  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(false);
  const [color, setColor] = useState('#fbbf24');
  const [points, setPoints] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const activate = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(true);
  }, []);

  const deactivate = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(false);
    setPoints([]);
  }, []);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    setPoints([]);
  }, []);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(false);
    setPoints([]);
    setColor('#fbbf24');
  }, []);

  const handleClick = useCallback((pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }): boolean => {
    if (!isActive) return false;

    const target = findShapeAt(slide, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y);

    // If clicked on shape/fill - fill it
    if (target && (target.type === 'shape' || target.type === 'fill')) {
      updateElement(target.id, { fill: color });
      return true;
    }

    // Otherwise add point to polygon
    setPoints((prev) => [...prev, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y]);
    return true;
  }, [isActive, color, slide, updateElement]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    if (points.length < 6) {
      setPoints([]);
      return;
    }

    // Calculate bounding box
    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i += 2) {
      xs.push(points[i]);
      ys.push(points[i + 1]);
    }

    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);

    // Convert to relative coordinates
    const relativePoints = points.map((v, i) =>
      i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY
    );

    const fillElement: FillElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'fill',
      x: minX,
      y: minY,
      width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
      height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
      rotation: 0,
      points: relativePoints,
      fill: color,
      stroke: '',
      strokeWidth: 0,
      opacity: 1,
    };

    addElement(fillElement);
    setPoints([]);
  }, [points, color, addElement]);

  return {
    isActive,
    color,
    points,
    activate,
    deactivate,
    setColor,
    handleClick,
    closePolygon,
    cancel,
    reset,
  };
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';

interface WorkerResult {
  key: string;
  url: string | null;
}

export function useFillWorker() {
  const workerRef = useRef<Worker | null>(null);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<WorkerResult | null>(null);
  const pendingKeyRef = useRef<string>('');
  const seqRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const worker = new Worker(
      new URL('../../workers/fillWorker.ts', import.meta.url),
      { type: 'module' },
    );

    worker.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<{ seq: number; url: string | null }>) => {
      if (e.data.seq !== seqRef.current) return;
      setResult({ key: pendingKeyRef.current, url: e.data.url });
    };

    worker.onerror = (err) => {
      console.error('[fillWorker] error', err);
    };

    workerRef.current = worker;
    return () => {
      worker.terminate();
      workerRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  const rasterize = useCallback(
    (
      key: string,
      width: number,
      height: number,
      shapes: StampShapeItem[],
      fills: FillSpec[],
    ) => {
      const worker = workerRef.current;
      if (!worker) return;

      const seq = ++seqRef.current;
      pendingKeyRef.current = key;

      worker.postMessage({ seq, width, height, shapes, fills });
    },
    [],
  );

  return { result, rasterize };
}

/**
 * Group Drag Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle dragging multiple selected elements
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface DragState {
  isDragging: boolean;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
}

interface UseGroupDragReturn {
  dragState: DragState;
  startDrag: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  updateDrag: (x: number, y: number) => { dx: number; dy: number } | null;
  endDrag: () => void;
}

export function useGroupDrag(): UseGroupDragReturn {
  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<DragState>({
    isDragging: false,
    startX: 0,
    startY: 0,
  });
  
  const lastPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const startDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: true,
      startX: x,
      startY: y,
    });
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
  }, []);

  const updateDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!dragState.isDragging) return null;
    
    const dx = x - lastPosRef.current.x;
    const dy = y - lastPosRef.current.y;
    
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
    
    return { dx, dy };
  }, [dragState.isDragging]);

  const endDrag = useCallback(() => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: false,
      startX: 0,
      startY: 0,
    });
  }, []);

  return {
    dragState,
    startDrag,
    updateDrag,
    endDrag,
  };
}

/**
 * History hook - Presentation Layer
 * Undo/redo functionality with Immer
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { produce } from 'immer';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import { MAX_HISTORY } from '../../shared/constants';

export interface HistoryEntry {
  readonly slides: Slide[];
  readonly activeSlideId: string | null;
}

export interface UseHistoryReturn {
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
  push: (slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => void;
  undo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  redo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useHistory(): UseHistoryReturn {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(-1);
  const lastCommittedRef = useRef<Slide[] | null>(null);

  const push = useCallback(
    (slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => {
      const last = lastCommittedRef.current;

      // Skip if same reference
      if (last === slides) return;

      // Structural sharing with Immer
      const snapshot = last
        ? produce(last, (draft) => {
            if (draft.length !== slides.length) {
              draft.length = slides.length;
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) {
              if (draft[i] !== slides[i]) draft[i] = slides[i];
            }
          })
        : slides;

      lastCommittedRef.current = snapshot;

      setEntries((prev) => {
        const trimmed = prev.slice(0, index + 1);
        trimmed.push({ slides: snapshot, activeSlideId });
        while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
        return trimmed;
      });

      setIndex((prev) => {
        const next = prev + 1;
        return next >= MAX_HISTORY ? MAX_HISTORY - 1 : next;
      });
    },
    [index]
  );

  const undo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index <= 0) return null;
    const prev = entries[index - 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = prev.slides;
    setIndex(index - 1);
    return prev;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const redo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index >= entries.length - 1) return null;
    const next = entries[index + 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = next.slides;
    setIndex(index + 1);
    return next;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setEntries([]);
    setIndex(-1);
    lastCommittedRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  return {
    canUndo: index > 0,
    canRedo: index < entries.length - 1,
    push,
    undo,
    redo,
    reset,
  };
}

/**
 * Import/Export Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle file operations
 */

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

interface ExportData {
  version: string;
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
}

export function useIO() {
  const exportJSON = useCallback((slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => {
    const exportData: ExportData = {
      version: '2.0',
      slides,
      data,
    };

    const json = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, []);

  const importJSON = useCallback((
    file: File,
    onSuccess: (slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const content = e.target?.result as string;
        const data = JSON.parse(content);
        
        // Validate structure
        if (!data.slides || !Array.isArray(data.slides)) {
          throw new Error('Invalid file format: missing slides');
        }
        
        // Migrate if needed
        const version = data.version || '1.0';
        const slides = migrateSlides(data.slides, version);
        const dataRoots = data.data || [];
        
        onSuccess(slides, dataRoots);
      } catch (err) {
        onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to import file');
      }
    };
    
    reader.onerror = () => {
      onError('Failed to read file');
    };
    
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }, []);

  const exportSVG = useCallback((slide: Slide) => {
    // Generate SVG from slide
    const svg = generateSVG(slide);
    const blob = new Blob([svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `slide-${slide.name}-${Date.now()}.svg`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, []);

  const exportServerSVG = useCallback(async (
    slides: Slide[],
    onSuccess: () => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/export/svg', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ slides }),
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Server export failed');
      }
      
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `export-${Date.now()}.svg`;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      
      onSuccess();
    } catch (err) {
      onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Export failed');
    }
  }, []);

  const exportServerPDF = useCallback(async (
    slides: Slide[],
    onSuccess: () => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/export/pdf', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ slides }),
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Server export failed');
      }
      
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `export-${Date.now()}.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      
      onSuccess();
    } catch (err) {
      onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Export failed');
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    exportJSON,
    importJSON,
    exportSVG,
    exportServerSVG,
    exportServerPDF,
  };
}

// Migration helpers
function migrateSlides(slides: unknown[], version: string): Slide[] {
  // Simple migration - in real app would have complex logic
  if (version === '1.0') {
    // Migrate from v1.0 to v2.0
    return slides.map((slide: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
      ...slide,
      elements: (slide.elements as unknown[] || []).map((el: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
        ...el,
        rotation: el.rotation || 0,
      })),
    })) as Slide[];
  }
  
  return slides as Slide[];
}

// SVG generation
function generateSVG(slide: Slide): string {
  const elements = slide.elements.map((el) => {
    switch (el.type) {
      case 'rect':
      case 'shape':
        return `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${(el as { fill?: string }).fill || 'none'}" />`;
      case 'text':
        return `<text x="${el.x}" y="${el.y + (el as { fontSize?: number }).fontSize || 16}">${(el as { text?: string }).text || ''}</text>`;
      default:
        return '';
    }
  }).join('\n');

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<svg width="${slide.width}" height="${slide.height}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
  <rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="${slide.background}" />
  ${elements}
</svg>`;
}

/**
 * Rubber Band (Lasso) Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle rectangular selection on canvas
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface Point { x: number; y: number }

interface RubberBandState {
  isActive: boolean;
  start: Point | null;
  current: Point | null;
}

interface UseRubberBandReturn {
  rubberBand: RubberBandState;
  handleMouseDown: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseMove: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseUp: () => { start: Point; end: Point } | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useRubberBand(): UseRubberBandReturn {
  const [state, setState] = useState<RubberBandState>({
    isActive: false,
    start: null,
    current: null,
  });
  
  const startRef = useRef<Point | null>(null);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    startRef.current = pos;
    setState({
      isActive: true,
      start: pos,
      current: pos,
    });
  }, []);

  const handleMouseMove = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    if (!state.isActive) return;
    setState(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      current: pos,
    }));
  }, [state.isActive]);

  const handleMouseUp = useCallback(() => {
    if (!state.isActive || !state.start || !state.current) {
      return null;
    }
    
    const result = {
      start: state.start,
      end: state.current,
    };
    
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
    
    return result;
  }, [state.isActive, state.start, state.current]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  return {
    rubberBand: state,
    handleMouseDown,
    handleMouseMove,
    handleMouseUp,
    reset,
  };
}

/**
 * Selection hook - Presentation Layer
 * Manages element selection state
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export interface UseSelectionReturn {
  selectedIds: readonly string[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  isSelected: (id: string) => boolean;
  select: (id: string, multi?: boolean) => void;
  selectMany: (ids: string[]) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
  toggleSelection: (id: string) => void;
}

export function useSelection(elements: CanvasElement[]): UseSelectionReturn {
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const selectedId = useMemo(() => selectedIds[0] ?? null, [selectedIds]);

  const selectedElements = useMemo(() => {
    return elements.filter((el) => selectedIds.includes(el.id));
  }, [elements, selectedIds]);

  const isSelected = useCallback(
    (id: string) => selectedIds.includes(id),
    [selectedIds]
  );

  const select = useCallback((id: string, multi = false) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      if (!multi) return [id];
      return prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id];
    });
  }, []);

  const selectMany = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
    setSelectedIds(ids);
  }, []);

  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedIds([]);
  }, []);

  const toggleSelection = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  }, []);

  return {
    selectedIds,
    selectedId,
    selectedElements,
    isSelected,
    select,
    selectMany,
    clearSelection,
    toggleSelection,
  };
}

/**
 * React hook for slides - Presentation Layer
 * Clean interface for components
 */

import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import { slideStore, useSlideStore, useActiveSlide } from '../../infrastructure/store/SlideStoreImpl';
import { createSlideUseCases } from '../../application/use-cases/SlideUseCases';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation, CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

const useCases = createSlideUseCases(slideStore);

export interface UseSlidesReturn {
  // State
  slides: Slide[];
  activeSlide: Slide | null;
  activeSlideId: string | null;

  // Actions
  addSlide: () => Slide;
  removeSlide: (id: string) => void;
  duplicateSlide: (id: string) => Slide | null;
  setActiveSlide: (id: string) => void;
  setSlides: (slides: Slide[]) => void;
  changeFormat: (format: DocFormat) => void;
  changeOrientation: (orientation: Orientation) => void;
  changeCustomSize: (width: number, height: number) => void;

  // Element actions
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  updateElement: (elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  removeElement: (elementId: string) => void;
  moveElementLayer: (elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1) => void;
}

export function useSlides(): UseSlidesReturn {
  const state = useSlideStore();
  const activeSlide = useActiveSlide();

  const slides = useMemo(() => state.slides, [state.slides]);
  const activeSlideId = useMemo(() => state.activeSlideId, [state.activeSlideId]);

  const addSlide = useCallback(() => {
    return useCases.createSlide({
      format: activeSlide?.format,
      orientation: activeSlide?.orientation,
    });
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const removeSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    useCases.removeSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const duplicateSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    return useCases.duplicateSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const setActiveSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    useCases.setActiveSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const setSlides = useCallback((newSlides: Slide[]) => {
    slideStore.setSlides(newSlides);
  }, []);

  const changeFormat = useCallback((format: DocFormat) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.changeFormat(activeSlide.id, format);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const changeOrientation = useCallback((orientation: Orientation) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.changeOrientation(activeSlide.id, orientation);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const changeCustomSize = useCallback((width: number, height: number) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    activeSlide.changeFormat('custom', { w: width, h: height });
    slideStore.updateSlide(activeSlide.id, activeSlide);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const addElement = useCallback((element: CanvasElement) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.addElement(activeSlide.id, element);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const updateElement = useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.updateElement({
      slideId: activeSlide.id,
      elementId,
      patch,
    });
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const removeElement = useCallback((elementId: string) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.removeElement(activeSlide.id, elementId);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const moveElementLayer = useCallback((elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.moveElementLayer(activeSlide.id, elementId, direction);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  return {
    slides,
    activeSlide,
    activeSlideId,
    addSlide,
    removeSlide,
    duplicateSlide,
    setActiveSlide,
    setSlides,
    changeFormat,
    changeOrientation,
    changeCustomSize,
    addElement,
    updateElement,
    removeElement,
    moveElementLayer,
  };
}

/**
 * Stamp Fill Mode Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle fill creation in stamp editor
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { findShapesContaining } from '../../lib/stamps/geometry';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface UseStampFillModeOptions {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export interface UseStampFillModeReturn {
  fillMode: boolean;
  setFillMode: (v: boolean) => void;
  fillColor: string;
  setFillColor: (v: string) => void;
  handleCanvasClick: (px: number, py: number) => void;
}

export function useStampFillMode({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height,
  commit, select,
}: UseStampFillModeOptions): UseStampFillModeReturn {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    if (!fillMode) return;

    console.log('[useStampFillMode] Click at:', px, py);
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] Available shapes:', shapes.filter(s => s.kind !== 'fill' && s.kind !== 'line').map(s => ({ id: s.id, kind: s.kind, x: s.x, y: s.y, w: s.width, h: s.height })));

    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] Containing shapes:', containing.length, containing.map(s => s.id));
    
    if (containing.length === 0) return;

    // Smallest containing shape = target
    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );

    // Local anchor coordinates (0..1)
    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      kind: 'fill',
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width, height,
      rotation: 0,
      cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor,
      fillTargetShapeId: target.id,
      fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
    };
    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return {
    fillMode, setFillMode,
    fillColor, setFillColor,
    handleCanvasClick,
  };
}

/**
 * Editor Context Provider - Presentation Layer
 * Provides editor state to child components
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { UseSlidesReturn } from '../hooks/useSlides';
import type { UseSelectionReturn } from '../hooks/useSelection';
import type { UseHistoryReturn } from '../hooks/useHistory';

interface EditorContextValue {
  slides: UseSlidesReturn;
  selection: UseSelectionReturn;
  history: UseHistoryReturn;
}

const EditorContext = createContext<EditorContextValue | null>(null);

interface EditorProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  slides: UseSlidesReturn;
  selection: UseSelectionReturn;
  history: UseHistoryReturn;
}

export const EditorProvider: React.FC<EditorProviderProps> = ({
  children,
  slides,
  selection,
  history,
}) => {
  const value = React.useMemo(
    () => ({
      slides,
      selection,
      history,
    }),
    [slides, selection, history]
  );

  return (
    <EditorContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </EditorContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useEditorContext(): EditorContextValue {
  const context = useContext(EditorContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useEditorContext must be used within EditorProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

// Convenience hooks
export function useSlidesContext(): UseSlidesReturn {
  return useEditorContext().slides;
}

export function useSelectionContext(): UseSelectionReturn {
  return useEditorContext().selection;
}

export function useHistoryContext(): UseHistoryReturn {
  return useEditorContext().history;
}

/**
 * Snap Service
 * Handles snapping to grid, elements, and guides
 */

import type { Slide } from '../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../shared/constants';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

export interface SnapOptions {
  snapToGrid?: boolean;
  snapToElements?: boolean;
  snapToCenter?: boolean;
  snapToEdges?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Snap position to grid
 */
export function snapToGrid(x: number, y: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  return {
    x: Math.round(x / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE,
    y: Math.round(y / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE,
  };
}

/**
 * Check if value is within threshold
 */
function isNear(value: number, target: number, threshold: number): boolean {
  return Math.abs(value - target) <= threshold;
}

/**
 * Compute snap for element drag
 */
export function computeSnap(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  options: SnapOptions = {}
): SnapResult {
  const { snapToGrid = true, snapToElements = true, snapToCenter = true, snapToEdges = true } = options;

  const result: SnapResult = {
    x,
    y,
    v: [],
    h: [],
  };

  // Snap to grid
  if (snapToGrid) {
    const gridX = Math.round(x / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;
    const gridY = Math.round(y / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

    if (isNear(x, gridX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.x = gridX;
      result.v.push(gridX);
    }
    if (isNear(y, gridY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.y = gridY;
      result.h.push(gridY);
    }
  }

  // Get other elements for snapping
  const otherElements = slide.elements.filter((e) => e.id !== element.id);

  for (const other of otherElements) {
    // Snap to edges
    if (snapToEdges) {
      // Left edge to left edge
      if (isNear(x, other.x, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x;
        result.v.push(other.x);
      }
      // Left edge to right edge
      if (isNear(x, other.x + other.width, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x + other.width;
        result.v.push(other.x + other.width);
      }
      // Right edge to left edge
      if (isNear(x + element.width, other.x, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x - element.width;
        result.v.push(other.x);
      }
      // Right edge to right edge
      if (isNear(x + element.width, other.x + other.width, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x + other.width - element.width;
        result.v.push(other.x + other.width);
      }

      // Top edge to top edge
      if (isNear(y, other.y, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y;
        result.h.push(other.y);
      }
      // Top edge to bottom edge
      if (isNear(y, other.y + other.height, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y + other.height;
        result.h.push(other.y + other.height);
      }
      // Bottom edge to top edge
      if (isNear(y + element.height, other.y, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y - element.height;
        result.h.push(other.y);
      }
      // Bottom edge to bottom edge
      if (isNear(y + element.height, other.y + other.height, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y + other.height - element.height;
        result.h.push(other.y + other.height);
      }
    }

    // Snap to center
    if (snapToCenter) {
      const otherCenterX = other.x + other.width / 2;
      const otherCenterY = other.y + other.height / 2;
      const elementCenterX = x + element.width / 2;
      const elementCenterY = y + element.height / 2;

      // Horizontal center alignment
      if (isNear(elementCenterX, otherCenterX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = otherCenterX - element.width / 2;
        result.v.push(otherCenterX);
      }

      // Vertical center alignment
      if (isNear(elementCenterY, otherCenterY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = otherCenterY - element.height / 2;
        result.h.push(otherCenterY);
      }
    }
  }

  // Snap to slide center
  if (snapToCenter) {
    const slideCenterX = slide.width / 2;
    const slideCenterY = slide.height / 2;
    const elementCenterX = x + element.width / 2;
    const elementCenterY = y + element.height / 2;

    if (isNear(elementCenterX, slideCenterX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.x = slideCenterX - element.width / 2;
      result.v.push(slideCenterX);
    }
    if (isNear(elementCenterY, slideCenterY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.y = slideCenterY - element.height / 2;
      result.h.push(slideCenterY);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * Get snap guides for display
 */
export function getSnapGuides(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide
): { v: number[]; h: number[] } {
  const snap = computeSnap(element, x, y, slide);
  return {
    v: snap.v,
    h: snap.h,
  };
}

/**
 * Check if snap is active
 */
export function isSnapActive(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide
): boolean {
  const snap = computeSnap(element, x, y, slide);
  return snap.x !== x || snap.y !== y;
}

/**
 * Core domain types - strictly typed, no any
 * Based on Clean Architecture principles
 */

// ============================================
// Value Objects
// ============================================

export type StampShape = 'circle' | 'oval' | 'rect' | 'triangle';
export type DashType = 'solid' | 'dashed' | 'dotted' | 'dashdot' | 'longdash';
export type BasicShape = 'rect' | 'roundRect' | 'circle' | 'triangle' | 'star' | 'arrow';
export type TextAlign = 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
export type TextVerticalAlign = 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
export type Orientation = 'portrait' | 'landscape';
export type DocFormat = 'a4' | 'a5' | 'a6' | 'letter' | 'ticket' | 'card' | 'custom';
export type DataFieldType = 'string' | 'number' | 'date' | 'boolean' | 'image' | 'object' | 'array';
export type AnchorSide = 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
export type ConnectorRouting = 'straight' | 'orthogonal';
export type StampShapeKind = 'circle' | 'ellipse' | 'rect' | 'triangle' | 'line' | 'fill';
export type StampFillMode = 'subtract' | 'intersect';
export type ImageFit = 'contain' | 'cover' | 'fill';

export interface Padding {
  readonly top: number;
  readonly right: number;
  readonly bottom: number;
  readonly left: number;
}

export interface Position {
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
}

export interface Size {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
}

export interface Bounds extends Position, Size {}

export interface Color {
  readonly r: number;
  readonly g: number;
  readonly b: number;
  readonly a?: number;
}

// ============================================
// Data Schema
// ============================================

export interface DataField {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly type: DataFieldType;
  readonly sample: string;
  readonly children?: readonly DataField[];
  readonly sampleCount?: number;
}

export interface DataRoot {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly fields: readonly DataField[];
}

// Element Types (Discriminated Unions)
// ============================================

export interface BaseElement extends Bounds {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly rotation: number;
  readonly locked?: boolean;
}

export interface TextElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'text';
  readonly text: string;
  readonly html?: string;
  readonly richText: boolean;
  readonly fontSize: number;
  readonly fontFamily: string;
  readonly fill: string;
  readonly fontStyle: string;
  readonly textDecoration: string;
  readonly align: TextAlign;
  readonly verticalAlign: TextVerticalAlign;
  readonly lineHeight: number;
  readonly padding: Padding;
  readonly background: string;
}

export interface ImageElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'image';
  readonly src: string;
}

export interface ShapeElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'shape';
  readonly shape: BasicShape;
  readonly fill: string;
  readonly stroke: string;
  readonly strokeWidth: number;
  readonly text: string;
  readonly fontSize: number;
  readonly textFill: string;

  readonly textAlign?: TextAlign;
  readonly textVerticalAlign?: TextVerticalAlign;
  readonly textPadding?: Padding;
  readonly textFontFamily?: string;
  readonly textLineHeight?: number;
  readonly textFontStyle?: string;
  readonly textDecoration?: string;
}

export interface LineElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'line';
  readonly points: readonly number[];
  readonly stroke: string;
  readonly strokeWidth: number;
  readonly dash: DashType;
  readonly isArrow: boolean;
}

export interface TableElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'table';
  readonly rows: number;
  readonly cols: number;
  readonly data: readonly (readonly string[])[];
  readonly borderColor: string;
  readonly headerBg: string;
  readonly fontSize: number;
}

  readonly type: 'footer';
  readonly text: string;
  readonly html?: string;
  readonly fill: string;
  readonly bgColor: string;
  readonly fontSize: number;
}

export interface PlaceholderElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'placeholder';
  readonly fieldPath: string;
  readonly fieldType: DataFieldType;
  readonly sample: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly inline?: boolean;
  readonly fontSize: number;
  readonly fontFamily: string;
  readonly fill: string;
  readonly fontStyle: string;
  readonly textDecoration: string;
  readonly align: TextAlign;
  readonly verticalAlign: TextVerticalAlign;
  readonly lineHeight: number;
  readonly padding: Padding;
  readonly background: string;
  readonly imageMode?: 'fit' | 'fill';
  readonly html?: string;
}

export interface FillElement extends BaseElement {

  readonly points: readonly number[];
  readonly fill: string;
  readonly stroke: string;
  readonly strokeWidth: number;
  readonly opacity: number;
}

export interface DiagramElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'diagram';
  readonly svg: string;
  readonly xml: string;
}

export interface ConnectorElement extends BaseElement {
  readonly type: 'connector';
  readonly fromId: string;
  readonly fromAnchor: AnchorSide;
  readonly toId: string;
  readonly toAnchor: AnchorSide;
  readonly stroke: string;
  readonly strokeWidth: number;
  readonly dash: DashType;

  readonly label?: string;
  readonly labelFontSize?: number;
  readonly labelFill?: string;
  readonly labelOffsetX?: number;
  readonly labelOffsetY?: number;
}

// Stamp Types
export interface StampShapeItem {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: StampShapeKind;
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly rotation?: number;
  readonly cornerRadius: number;
  readonly points?: readonly number[];
  readonly stroke: string;
  readonly strokeWidth: number;
  readonly dash: DashType;

  | TextElement
  | ImageElement
  | ShapeElement
  | LineElement
  | TableElement
  | FooterElement
  | PlaceholderElement
  | FillElement
  | DiagramElement
  | ConnectorElement
  | StampElement;

// Type guards
export const isTextElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is TextElement => el.type === 'text';
export const isImageElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ImageElement => el.type === 'image';
export const isShapeElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ShapeElement => el.type === 'shape';
export const isLineElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is LineElement => el.type === 'line';
export const isTableElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is TableElement => el.type === 'table';
export const isFooterElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is FooterElement => el.type === 'footer';
export const isPlaceholderElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is PlaceholderElement => el.type === 'placeholder';
export const isFillElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is FillElement => el.type === 'fill';
export const isDiagramElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is DiagramElement => el.type === 'diagram';
export const isConnectorElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ConnectorElement => el.type === 'connector';
export const isStampElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is StampElement => el.type === 'stamp';

/**
 * ID generation utilities
 */

let counter = 0;

/**
 * Generate unique ID
 */
export function generateId(): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  const count = (counter++).toString(36);
  return `${timestamp}-${random}-${count}`;
}

/**
 * Generate ID with prefix
 */
export function generateIdWithPrefix(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}-${generateId()}`;
}

/**
 * Check if string is valid ID
 */
export function isValidId(id: string): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0;
}

  name: string,
  description: string,
  category: TemplateBundle['category'],
  slides: Slide[]
): TemplateBundle {
  return {
    id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    description,
    category,
    slides: [...slides],
  };
}

FILE: workers\fillWorker.ts

/// <reference lib="webworker" />
import { rasterizeCompositeFills, type FillSpec } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../shared/types';

interface Request {
  seq: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

self.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<Request>) => {
  const { seq, width, height, shapes, fills } = e.data;
  try {
    const url = rasterizeCompositeFills({ width, height, shapes, fills });
    self.postMessage({ seq, url });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[fillWorker] rasterize failed', err);
    self.postMessage({ seq, url: null });
  }
};
